A topology-aware gate dynamically routes each node--time instance to a sparse subset of experts, constructing a personalized mixed-curvature representation. To ensure parameter efficiency and training stability under extreme label scarcity, CurvPrompt employs soft routing during pre-training to build a continuous topology--geometry mapping, and transitions to hard Top-K routing with uniform weights during downstream adaptation. Extensive experiments across four benchmark datasets show that CurvPrompt significantly advances few-shot link prediction while delivering strong, consistent performance on node classification tasks, validating the necessity of geometry-adaptive prompting.

</details>


### [161] [Does Latent Context Help? A Controlled Evaluation of Inverse Reinforcement Learning in Arctic Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06105)
*Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Dilith Jayakody,Biruk Ambaw,Jaswanth Kumar,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在3,186条AIS船船航迹上，对线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励和带潜在上下文的元IRL模型进行了受控评估，发现非线性奖励比线性基线提升50.9%的留出似然，而添加船舶特定潜在上下文反而使性能下降16.5%，表明可观测的路线和环境条件已能解释大部分行为变异，隐含偏好并非真的是"隐藏"因素驱动的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助导航在北极航运中需要可解释且对环境变化鲁棒的奖励模型。元IRL方法通过引入潜在上下文变量来捕捉行为异质性，但现有研究不清楚这些潜变量是否真正恢复了隐藏偏好，还是仅仅重新编码了状态中已可观测的信息。本文旨在通过受控实验厘清这一问题。

Method: 基于9个北极航运季节、202艘船的3,186条AIS航迹数据，构建三种模型进行对比评估：(1)线性共享奖励模型；(2)非线性共享奖励模型；(3)在相同非线性架构上叠加船舶特定潜在上下文的元IRL模型。评估方法包括留出似然对比、行为分析、上下文探针以及预注册的特征隐藏消融实验，并从预测精度、路径保真度和奖励迁移三个维度进行模型排名。

Result: 非线性奖励模型在留出似然上比线性基线提高50.9%；加入船舶潜在上下文后性能反而下降16.5%。行为分析、上下文探针和特征隐藏消融实验一致表明，表观上的船舶间行为差异主要由可观测的路线和环境条件解释，而非隐藏的船舶特定因素。此外，三个评估指标（预测精度、路径保真度、奖励迁移）产生了不同的模型排名，说明单一指标不足以全面评价学习奖励的质量。

Conclusion: 在向IRL模型添加逐船潜在上下文之前，应先检验可观测的路线、环境和船舶特征是否已能充分解释行为变异。这一发现对安全关键领域（如北极航运）的可信AI部署具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted navigation can help Arctic shipping adapt to rapidly changing sea-ice conditions, but reliable deployment requires reward models that are interpretable and robust to changing environments. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a framework for recovering such rewards from vessel trajectories, while recent meta-IRL methods introduce latent context variables to capture behavioral heterogeneity. However, it remains unclear whether these latent representations recover genuinely hidden preferences or simply re-encode information already available in the observed state. We conduct a controlled evaluation on 3,186 AIS-derived voyages from 202 vessels across nine Arctic shipping seasons, comparing a linear shared reward, a nonlinear shared reward, and a latent-context model built on the same nonlinear architecture. The nonlinear reward improves held-out likelihood by 50.9% over the linear baseline, whereas adding vessel-specific latent context reduces performance by 16.5%. Behavioral analysis, context probes, and a pre-registered feature-hiding ablation show that apparent vessel-level variation is largely explained by observable route and environmental conditions rather than hidden vessel-specific factors. Moreover, predictive accuracy, route fidelity, and reward transfer yield different model rankings, demonstrating that no single metric is sufficient to evaluate learned rewards. These findings motivate testing whether the observed route, environmental, and vessel features already explain behavioral variation before adding per-vessel latent context. This supports more trustworthy AI deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [162] [Kastor: An efficient fine-tuning strategy for generative emulation of PDE simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06107)
*Guillaume Couairon,Alexis Jacq,Yu-Han Wu,Renu Singh,Yana Hasson,Quentin Berthet,Romuald Elie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kastor，一种将确定性物理基础模型转化为高效生成式代理模型的综合方法，通过两阶段推理方案（大步长自回归+非因果时间超分辨）、均值预测正则化（MPR）和空间梯度匹配，显著提高了长时间预测准确性、谱一致性和计算效率，在The Well基准的10个数据集中有8个优于Walrus方法，平均预测误差降低42.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偏微分方程（PDE）求解器计算成本高昂，而标准自回归机器学习仿真器在长时间预测中会累积误差，且难以捕捉复杂物理系统的随机性。因此需要一种既能减少误差累积又能保持计算效率的生成式替代模型。

Method: 1. 两阶段推理方案：将大步长因果自回归模型与非因果时间超分辨率网络结合，减少误差累积并降低计算成本。2. 均值预测正则化（MPR）：一种新的训练目标，在空噪声条件下约束生成模型预测确定性分布均值，显著提升FGN和扩散模型仿真器的性能与稳定性。3. 空间梯度匹配：将梯度信息纳入训练，改善仿真精度和物理保真度（以功率谱密度衡量）。

Result: 在The Well基准的多种仿真数据集上，Kastor在预测精度、谱一致性和计算效率方面均优于竞争方法。平均预测误差相较基于Walrus微调方法降低42.9%，在10个数据集中的8个上取得了更低的方差归一化均方根误差（VRMSE）。

Conclusion: Kastor通过结合两阶段推理、均值预测正则化和空间梯度匹配，成功将确定性物理基础模型转化为高效且准确的生成式代理模型，在长时间预测、物理保真度和计算成本之间取得了优异的平衡，为物理仿真加速提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine learning offers a promising avenue to accelerate physical simulations by replacing computationally expensive traditional Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers with fast, differentiable surrogate models. However, standard auto-regressive ML emulators often suffer from error accumulation over long horizons and struggle to capture the stochasticity of complex physical systems. In this paper, we propose Kastor, a comprehensive methodology to adapt a deterministic physics foundation model into a highly efficient and accurate generative surrogate. First, we introduce a two-stage inference scheme that combines a large-stride causal auto-regressive model with a non-causal temporal super-resolution network, significantly reducing error accumulation while minimizing computational cost. Second, we present Mean prediction regularization (MPR), a novel training objective that constrains the generative model to predict the deterministic distribution mean under null noise conditioning. This regularization dramatically improves the performance and stability of both Functional Generative Networks (FGN) and diffusion-based emulators. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating spatial gradient matching improves the accuracy and physical fidelity of the simulations as measured by power spectrum density. Extensive evaluations on diverse simulation datasets of the benchmark The Well show that with these components, our model outperforms competing methods in forecasting accuracy, spectral consistency, and computational efficiency. Our model achieves a 42.9% average reduction in forecasting compared to our reference based on the Walrus finetuning methodology, and outperforms Walrus for 8 out of 10 datasets on variance-normalized RMSE (VRMSE).

</details>


### [163] [Is Self-Pretraining really useful to improve diagnosis in medical Time Series?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06122)
*Omar Coser,Antonio Orvieto,Paolo Soda,Loredana Zollo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Self-PreTraining (SPT) 对医学时间序列数据的transformer模型的影响，在三个医学任务（康复机器人、压力检测、帕金森检测）上验证了SPT能一致性地提升分类准确率0-6个百分点，且模型越深收益越大，表明SPT是一种简单通用的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期证据表明transformer架构在长上下文基准上受益于Self-PreTraining (SPT)，但尚不清楚这种收益能否扩展到多模态、多变量甚至简单的单变量医学时间序列数据。作者旨在评估SPT在有限数据条件下对医学时间序列transformer模型性能和可扩展性的影响。

Method: 在三个代表性医学时间序列任务（Camargo康复数据集、Non-EEG Stress压力检测、Gait Parkinson帕金森检测）上评估transformer架构。模型从零训练或通过SPT训练，使用四种基于掩码的自监督目标来促进时间和跨模态表征学习，并系统变化模型深度以检验容量与预训练收益之间的交互。

Result: SPT在所有数据集和配置上一致性地将分类准确率提升0-6个百分点，收益取决于掩码策略、数据集和架构。不仅在多变量设置中观察到收益，在限制为简单单变量输入时也同样有效。对于更深的模型，收益更大，因为深层模型能更好地利用预训练期间学习的丰富时间表征。

Conclusion: SPT是一种简单且通用的策略，无需任务特定的架构改动即可提升医学时间序列任务中transformer的性能，支持其在数据受限的临床环境中提升鲁棒性和准确性的潜力。

Abstract: Inspired by recent evidence that transformer architectures benefit from Self-PreTraining (SPT) on long-context benchmarks, we investigate whether similar gains extend to multimodal, multivariate, and even simple univariate medical time series. Our objective is to assess the impact of SPT on the performance and scalability of transformer-based models across diverse medical applications, particularly under limited data conditions. We evaluate transformer architectures on three representative medical time-series tasks: rehabilitation robotics (Camargo dataset), stress detection (Non-EEG Stress), and Parkinson's disease detection (Gait Parkinson's Disease). Models are trained either from scratch or through SPT using four masking-based objectives designed to promote temporal and cross-modal representation learning, and we systematically vary model depth to examine how capacity interacts with pre-training benefits. Across datasets and configurations, SPT consistently improves classification accuracy by 0-6 percentage points depending on masking strategy, dataset and architecture, with gains observed not only in multivariate settings but also when models are restricted to simple univariate inputs. The improvements increase for deeper models that can better exploit the enriched temporal representations learned during pre-training. These findings indicate that SPT is a simple and general strategy that enhances transformer performance on medical time-series tasks without requiring task-specific architectural changes, supporting its potential to improve robustness and accuracy in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [164] [LLM Inference Under Bursty Workload Distribution: Modifying the WAIT Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06135)
*Anjali Gangadhar Katageria,Shobha Rani,Raghu Nandan Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型推理调度中假设恒定请求到达率的不足，提出了一种对WAIT算法的轻量级扩展，通过在线估计请求强度来适应时变的突发流量，在低到达率偏移场景下实现了比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM更高的吞吐量且延迟相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调度算法通常假设请求遵循恒定速率的泊松到达过程，但这无法反映真实世界中流量固有的突发性和动态变化特征，导致调度效率在实际场景中不理想。

Method: 在WAIT算法基础上提出轻量级扩展，无需先验流量知识，通过观察请求到达间隔时间进行在线估计请求强度，从而自适应时变的到达率。

Result: 基于MMPP合成工作负载（包含多种请求类型）的仿真评估表明，在低到达率偏移场景下，提出的方法相比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM实现了更高的吞吐量，同时保持相当的延迟。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级扩展能有效应对真实世界流量的突发性和时变性，在不牺牲延迟的情况下提升LLM推理系统的吞吐量。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT and Claude are widely used for information retrieval and problem-solving. Recent work has focused on improving scheduling algorithms to boost throughput while maintaining low latency. However, these approaches often assume Poisson request arrivals with constant rates - an assumption that fails to reflect the inherently bursty and dynamic nature of real-world traffic. We propose a lightweight extension to the state-of-the-art WAIT algorithm [1], which adapts to time-varying arrival rates without prior traffic knowledge. The proposed algorithm performs online estimation of request intensity based on observed interarrival times. Using Markov Modulated Poisson Process (MMPP)-based synthetic workloads with diverse request types, we conduct a simulation-based evaluation demonstrating that the proposed method achieves higher throughput than Sarathi-Serve [2], ORCA [3], and vLLM [4] in the evaluated low arrival-rate shift scenarios while maintaining comparable latency.

</details>


### [165] [SkillTFM: Gated Skill Evolution for Training-Free Adaptation of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06137)
*Yi He,Zhengkang Guan,Anpeng Wu,Peng Cui,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillTFM是一个无需训练的表格基础模型（TFM）适应系统，通过门控智能体技能演化替代传统参数更新，在不进行微调的情况下提升TFM在分布漂移和异构特征场景下的预测性能，实验中AUC提升0.128-0.142，非线性边界AUC从0.699提升至0.898。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据在真实应用中广泛存在，表格基础模型（TFMs）虽提供通用预测能力，但其实际部署受到分布漂移、异构特征语义和任务特定模式的制约，传统解决方案依赖代价高昂的微调或额外标注数据。

Method: SkillTFM提出一种训练-free系统，将TFM适应从参数更新转变为门控的智能体技能演化。核心是可验证且可扩展的技能库，包含两个关键组件：边界证据识别（刻画任务结构和基模型失败模式）与门控技能演化（在显式验证约束下检索和扩展现有技能）。

Result: 在模拟边界设置和真实电力价格预测任务中，SkillTFM将AUC提升0.128-0.142，将非线性边界AUC从0.699提升至0.898。跨不同TFM骨干网络的实验进一步验证了其有效性和泛化性。

Conclusion: SkillTFM证明通过技能演化的方式可以在无需训练和微调的情况下实现TFM的有效适应，为表格基础模型的实际部署提供了一种低成本、可扩展且具有良好泛化性的新范式。

Abstract: Tabular data are ubiquitous in real-world applications and are crucial for data-driven prediction and decision-making across science, industry, finance, healthcare, and public services. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose tabular learning, offering reusable predictors across diverse datasets and substantially reducing the need for task-specific training, tuning, and model development. However, their practical deployment remains constrained by distribution shifts, heterogeneous feature semantics, and task-specific patterns that are difficult to capture without costly fine-tuning or additional labeled data.
  To this end, we propose SkillTFM, a training-free system that shifts TFM adaptation from parameter updates to the gated evolution of agentic skills. The core of SkillTFM is a verifiable and extensible skill bank that couples boundary evidence identification with gated skill evolution: the former characterizes task structure and base-model failure patterns, whereas the latter retrieves and extends reusable skills subject to explicit validation. Across simulated boundary settings and real-world electricity-price forecasting, SkillTFM improves AUC by 0.128--0.142, raises nonlinear-boundary AUC from 0.699 to 0.898. Furthermore, experiments across TFM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SkillTFM.

</details>


### [166] [Threshold-Based Early Stopping of Accumulations in Neural Networks with Binary Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06177)
*Quentin Luquet de Saint-Germain,Massil Ait Abdeslam,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对二值神经网络提出一种后训练早期停止机制：利用训练集上累加部分和的统计特征，提前预测最终符号，从而跳过大量不必要的累加运算，在几乎不损失精度的前提下显著减少计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值神经网络中虽然点积变为符号控制的加减法，但操作数量并未减少——每个神经元仍需累加全部输入。然而累加过程中部分和的符号往往在很早就已确定，后续贡献只改变数值不改变最终输出激活，存在大量浪费计算。

Method: 提出一种后训练早期停止机制：在训练数据集上刻画运行累加和的行为特征，利用这些统计信息尽早预测最终符号。无需重训任何模型参数，且在权重理想排序下统计操作次数。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上的VGG11实验中，最深层卷积可去除86.6%的累加项，精度仅下降0.37个百分点；同时对三个最深卷积联合使用时，可减少全网络25%的算术运算，精度下降1.36个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法证明二值神经网络中大量累加运算在符号预测上本质是冗余的，通过后训练统计分析即可在不重训练的情况下大幅削减计算开销，为资源受限部署提供了一种有效的优化途径。

Abstract: Binary neural networks are very attractive for constrained deployment, enabling small footprint and low-power inference. For binary activations, the dot products become sign-controlled additions or subtractions, but the number of operations is unchanged. Indeed, every neuron or output channel still accumulates all of its input, even though only the sign will be retained, which is often wasteful. As the accumulation progresses, the running partial sum frequently drifts so far from zero that its final sign becomes highly predictable long before the last term is reached; every contribution evaluated after that point changes the value of the sum but not the final output activation. This paper turns this observation into a post-training early-stopping mechanism. We characterize the behavior of the running accumulations on the training dataset and use this information to predict the final sign as soon as possible. No model parameter is retrained. We count the number of operations under an idealized ordering of weights. On VGG11 applied to the CIFAR-10 dataset, the method removes $86.6\%$ of the accumulation terms of the deepest convolution for a $0.37$-point accuracy drop, and $25\%$ of the full-network arithmetic when used on the three deepest convolutions simultaneously, for a $1.36$-point drop.

</details>


### [167] [SAGA: Score-Weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment for Low-Resource Nordic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06179)
*Hoda Fakharzadehjahromy,Emil Wiman,Andreas Bueff,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Fredrik Heintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGA是一个解析器引导的偏好优化框架，用依赖解析器监督替代人工偏好标注，通过delta-DPO、复合奖励和奖励黑客监控在丹麦语、冰岛语和挪威语等低资源语言上显著提升语法质量，无需人类标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化在LLM中有效但依赖昂贵的人工偏好标注；在形态丰富且资源稀缺的语言中，人工标注难以获得，亟需一种不依赖人工标注的语法对齐方法。

Method: SAGA框架包括：1) 将解析器判断转化为偏好对用于delta-DPO训练；2) 将解析器质量与词汇多样性结合成复合奖励；3) 使用奖励差距准则过滤低信息量偏好对；4) 监控奖励黑客行为以维持可靠的监督信号。

Result: 在丹麦语、冰岛语和挪威语（使用GPT-SW3-1.3B）上，SAGA一致提升语法质量：丹麦语解析成功率从69.0%提升至93.8%；冰岛语在独立Stanza评估上提升+4.5个百分点（三次均值+3.3），母语者在80%成对比较中偏好SAGA输出；挪威语提升+28个百分点。

Conclusion: 在高品质依赖解析器可获取的低资源语言中，解析器派生的监督信号是人工偏好标注的实用替代方案，可用于语法对齐。

Abstract: Preference optimisation has proven effective for improving large language models but typically relies on costly human preference annotations. Extending these methods to morphologically rich, low-resource languages remains challenging because such annotations are scarce. We present SAGA (Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment), a parser-guided preference optimisation framework that replaces human labels with dependency-parser supervision. SAGA converts parser judgements into preference pairs for delta-DPO, combines parser quality with lexical diversity in a composite reward, filters low-information pairs using a reward-gap criterion, and monitors reward hacking to maintain reliable supervision. Across Danish, Icelandic, and Norwegian Bokmål using GPT-SW3-1.3B, SAGA consistently improves grammatical quality without requiring human preference labels. Danish parse success increases from 69.0% to 93.8%, Icelandic achieves a +4.5 percentage-point improvement on an independent Stanza evaluation (three-run mean +3.3 percentage points) while native speakers prefer SAGA outputs in 80% of pairwise comparisons, and Norwegian Bokmål improves by +28 percentage points. These results demonstrate that parser-derived supervision is a practical alternative to human preference annotation for grammatical alignment in low-resource languages where high-quality dependency parsers are available.

</details>


### [168] [Continual Learning in Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06216)
*Zhiyan Hou,Dan Zhang,Tao Feng,Liyuan Wang,Wei Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Hongyan An,Junfeng Fang,Haokai Ma,Zhaohui Xu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个三维框架（When、How、Where）来系统分析持续学习（CL）从参数中心化学习向系统级适应的范式转变，涵盖离策略/在策略/超梯度优化机制、训练前/训练后/推理时等多个阶段，以及内部参数与外部结构约束的更新位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习主要关注参数层面的机制（训练策略、架构设计、权重适应），但新兴范式正在重塑其范围——如on-policy学习扩展了更新机制空间，test-time training将CL扩展至推理阶段，而记忆、技能库和交互协议等外部组件进一步扩展了模型能力的演化边界。这些进展共同表明CL正从参数中心化学习向系统级适应转变，需要一种统一的框架来刻画这一转变。

Method: 作者构建了一个三轴分析框架来刻画持续学习的演化：(1) How维度——涵盖off-policy、on-policy以及超越梯度的优化机制；(2) When维度——涵盖预训练、后训练和推理时三个阶段的学习演化；(3) Where维度——区分内部参数更新与外部结构约束两种更新位置。基于此框架，系统梳理了代表性方法并追踪持续学习的范式迁移过程。

Result: 论文系统地调查了代表性持续学习方法，基于三轴框架追踪了持续学习从参数中心化到系统级适应的持续转变过程，并识别了这一范式转变带来的关键挑战、广泛影响和未来方向。

Conclusion: 持续学习正经历从参数中心化范式向系统级适应的范式转变，该转变可通过When（何时学习）、How（如何学习）、Where（何处学习）三个维度进行全面刻画。这一框架不仅有助于理解现有CL方法的谱系，也为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Classical continual learning (CL) has primarily focused on enabling models to update and retain knowledge through parameter-centric mechanisms, e.g., training strategies, architectural designs, and weight adaptation. However, emerging paradigms are reshaping the scope of CL beyond this traditional model adaptation view. For instance, on-policy learning broadens the space of update mechanisms; test-time training extends CL from the training phase to inference; and external harness components such as memory, skill libraries, and interaction protocols extend the evolutionary boundaries of model capabilities far beyond the static parameter space. Collectively, these developments indicate a transition from parameter-centric learning toward system-level adaptation. To characterize this transition, we examine the evolution of continual learning through three dimensions: When, How, and Where learning occurs. The How dimension encompasses off-policy, on-policy, and beyond-gradient optimization mechanics. The When dimension captures evolution across pre-training, post-training, and inference-time stages. The Where dimension delineates updates occurring within internal parameters versus external structural constraints. Anchored by this tri-axial framework, we systematically survey representative methods, trace the ongoing transition of continual learning, and discuss the key challenges, broader implications, and future directions arising from this paradigm shift.

</details>


### [169] [Timestep-Conditioned Transformers for Global Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06241)
*Sam Levang,Fran Bartolic,Ty Dickinson,Chase Dwelle,Paulius Rauba,Viktor Cikojevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM-3是一个轻量级概率全球天气预测模型（约134M参数），通过显式多时间步推理解决传统机器学习天气预报模型中步长选择的权衡问题。用户可在推理时配置模型步长，混合时间步训练还能提升rollout稳定性，实现了接近SOTA的中期概率预测技能、稳定的扩展范围rollout和高效训练推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型依赖固定且预设的自回归时间步长，存在根本性权衡：短时间步（1-6小时）能精细解析昼夜循环内的大气动力学，但会加剧给定预测时限内的误差累积；长时间步（24小时）虽减少误差累积，却限制了短期预测中亚日尺度高可预测性的利用。因此需要一个能同时兼顾预测精度和可用性的模型。

Method: GEM-3提出显式多时间步推断机制，使用单一训练权重集，推理时可配置模型步长以平衡预测能力和可用性。此外，采用混合时间步训练以提升rollout稳定性。模型内核为轻量级的邻域注意力变换器（neighborhood-attention transformer），约134M参数，基于等距柱状网格，并在前代GEM-2基础上引入了多项架构改进。

Result: 混合时间步训练相比时间步特化模型持续提升了rollout稳定性；GEM-3实现了接近SOTA的中期概率预测技能、稳定的扩展范围rollout、高效的训练与推理，并提供决策相关的诊断输出。

Conclusion: GEM-3通过显式多时间步推断有效解决了传统模型时间步长的权衡问题，构建了一个兼顾预测技能、rollout稳定性、计算效率和实际决策可用性的实用型天气预报系统。

Abstract: Existing machine-learning weather forecasting models rely on predetermined and fixed autoregressive timesteps. The choice of model timestep involves a fundamental trade-off: shorter timesteps (e.g. 1 to 6 hours) finely resolve atmospheric dynamics within the diurnal cycle but increase error accumulation for a given forecast horizon, while longer timesteps (e.g. 24 hours) reduce error accumulation but limit the usability of short-range forecasts where sub-daily predictability is high. In this work, we present GEM-3, a probabilistic global weather model that addresses this trade-off through explicit multi-timestep inference. With a single set of trained weights, the model timestep can be configured at inference time to balance predictability and usability across a broad forecast horizon. Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [170] [A Six-Dimensional Taxonomy of Post-Training Adaptation Techniques with Applications in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06246)
*Fardin Afdideh,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对机器学习中的post-training adaptation（训练后适应）文献进行了系统性综述，提出了一个六维分类体系（机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围和模型类型），并梳理了传统机器学习到多模态大语言模型各阶段中适应技术的演进关系与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后适应技术（如微调、参数高效适应、对齐、检索增强、模型编辑、反学习、校准、多模态指令微调等）已成为现代机器学习实践的核心，但相关文献在不同技术族、模型类别和部署场景之间高度碎片化，缺乏统一的比较框架，难以描述模型被修改的方式和程度。

Method: 作者对训练后适应相关文献进行综合综述，提出一个六维分类体系（taxonomy），维度包括机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围和模型类型；同时建立了技术间的关系图谱（包括继承、取代、混合和分层部署栈），并区分了常被混淆的概念（如微调、检索增强和提示）。

Result: 该综述提供了一个统一的词汇/语言框架，可支持技术文档编写、模型变更追踪和治理分析；同时揭示了适应技术从传统机器学习、深度学习、基础模型、大语言模型到多模态大语言模型的演化路径和技术间关系，并识别了评估、可复现性、持久推理时适应、反学习、多模态适应和治理感知工作流等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 训练后适应需要一个统一的分类和描述框架来弥合文献碎片化问题；论文提出的六维分类体系及相关关系映射为社区提供了有效的沟通与比较基础，但评估标准化、可复现性和治理感知工作流等关键问题仍有待解决。

Abstract: Post-training adaptation has become central to modern machine learning practice and includes techniques such as retraining, fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, alignment, retrieval augmentation, model editing, unlearning, calibration, and Multimodal Instruction Tuning. However, the literature remains fragmented across technique families, model classes, and deployment contexts, making it difficult to compare methods or describe how a trained model has been modified. This survey synthesizes the post-training adaptation literature and introduces a six-dimensional taxonomy organized by mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, and model type. The taxonomy distinguishes commonly conflated terms such as fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, and prompting, and shows how adaptation strategies evolve from traditional machine learning through deep learning, foundation models, large language models, and multimodal large language models. It also maps relationships among techniques, including inheritance, supersession, hybridization, and layered deployment stacks. The resulting vocabulary can support technical documentation, model-change tracking, and governance analysis. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in evaluation, reproducibility, persistent inference-time adaptation, unlearning, multimodal adaptation, and governance-aware post-training workflows.

</details>


### [171] [MetaboLLM: a metabolomics-specialized large language model for biochemical knowledge integration and predictive metabolite graph construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06253)
*Dohyun Ku,Min Gu Kwak,Francisco J. Pasquel,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了MetaboLLM（代谢组学专用大语言模型）和MetaboLLM-GIN（将生成的生化描述转化为代谢物图用于患者级预测的图同构网络）两个模型，在四种骨干模型上均优于基础和医学适配模型，并在应激性高血糖预测和绝经后激素方案分类两个临床任务中取得最佳AUC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢组学知识分散在不同的异构资源中，难以转化为可预测的表征。现有方法缺乏能够整合这些分散知识并将其应用于下游预测任务的统一框架。

Method: 采用持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索三个步骤构建代谢组学专用大语言模型MetaboLLM；进一步通过MetaboLLM-GIN将语言模型生成的生化描述转换为代谢物图结构，利用图同构网络（GIN）进行患者级预测。

Result: MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上均优于基线和医学适配模型，并成功迁移到外部公开基准。MetaboLLM-GIN在冠状动脉旁路移植术后应激性高血糖预测（AUC=0.8616）和绝经后激素方案分类（AUC=0.8123）中取得最高性能，优于常规模型、替代图构建方案及未适配或非检索的LLM配置，且模型解释产生了生物学上有意义的发现。

Conclusion: 领域专用语言模型能够将异构生化知识组织成可预测且可解释的代谢物图表征，为代谢组学的临床转化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Metabolomics knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous resources and remains difficult to translate into predictive representations. We developed MetaboLLM, a metabolomics-specialized large language model adapted through continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and structured retrieval, together with MetaboLLM-GIN, which converts generated biochemical descriptions into metabolite graphs for patient-level prediction using a graph isomorphism network. Across four backbone families, MetaboLLM outperformed corresponding base and medically adapted models on metabolomics knowledge, relational, and description tasks, and transferred to an external public benchmark. MetaboLLM-GIN achieved the highest AUC for stress hyperglycemia prediction after coronary artery bypass grafting (0.8616) and postmenopausal hormone-regimen classification (0.8123), outperforming conventional models, alternative graph constructions, and graphs generated from unadapted or non-retrieval LLM configurations. Model interpretation further produced biologically meaningful findings in both applications. These results show that domain-specialized language models can organize heterogeneous biochemical knowledge into predictive and interpretable metabolite graph representations.

</details>


### [172] [RxnCLF: Contrastive Transformation-Aware Reaction Foundation Model for Improved Reactivity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06259)
*Yiting Zheng,Cheng Fang,Anthony Donofrio,Haote Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RxnCLF，一个基于自监督对比学习的反应表示学习框架。它利用缩合反应图（CRG）将反应物和产物信息统一到单一图中，在170万条Pistachio反应数据上预训练，捕获反应中心和侧链上下文。在下游产率预测基准（包括Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化的BH偶联以及专有的HTE C-N偶联和酰胺形成数据集）上微调后，RxnCLF持续优于图基和序列基基线，取得最佳R²性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应产率预测面临标注数据稀缺和反应空间巨大且稀疏的双重挑战。现有的字符串、指纹和图基反应编码只能部分捕获化学转化信息，对复杂底物的反应预测准确性受限，亟需更强的反应表示学习方法。

Method: RxnCLF采用自监督对比学习框架，核心创新在于构建缩合反应图（CRG），将反应物和产物信息融合为单一图结构，使模型学习明确的反应转化结构而非分离的图。模型在170万条Pistachio反应上大规模预训练，学习紧凑且连续的反应潜空间，兼顾反应中心特征和侧链上下文，实现变换感知和化学可解释的表征。

Result: RxnCLF在多个产率预测基准上（Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化的BH偶联、专有HTE C-N偶联和酰胺形成数据集）微调后，持续优于图和序列基线方法，R²指标全面提升并取得最佳综合性能。

Conclusion: CRG-based RxnCLF展现出作为可扩展反应基础模型的潜力，有望推广到更广泛的反应空间，并支持区域选择性预测、对映选择性预测和反应条件优化等多样化的下游反应信息学任务。

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [173] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型评估中自适应测试（根据先前响应选择后续查询）与非自适应测试（预先固定所有查询）之间的能力差异。主要发现：非自适应可区分性要求分布类在条件概率上具有正分离度；当分离度为零时最优最坏情况误差恰为1/2。任何T查询自适应策略都可被O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))次非自适应随机化查询模拟（在总变差距离ρ内），且该二次方差距是最优的——最坏情况下自适应与非自适应查询复杂度之比为Θ(N²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型评估的核心问题：是否应当允许测试者根据模型先前响应动态调整后续测试？虽然交互式评估看似具有指数级分支优势，但其实际查询效率增益尚不清楚。作者希望通过理论刻画来回答：自适应测试相对于非自适应测试到底能带来多大的查询复杂度优势，以及两者的能力边界在哪里。

Method: 在有限结果空间上的条件查询模型中：1) 建立可区分性与条件概率分离度之间的等价关系，证明零分离时最优误差恰为1/2；2) 构造随机化非自适应策略，使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))对查询模拟任意T-查询自适应策略的转录，使总变差距离在ρ内；3) 构造自适应查询复杂度为常数而自适应查询复杂度为Ω(N²)的匹配分布族，据此确定自适应差距的精确阶为Θ(N²)。

Result: 学习性（可区分性）成立当且仅当两类分布的条件概率分离度为正；零分离下任意有限查询预算的最优最坏情况误差恰为1/2。非自适应模拟自适应查询的高效性由两个互补的结果界定：上界为O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))对查询，下界（匹配例）为Ω(N²)非自适应查询对应常数自适应查询，因此最坏情况下固定误差的自适应差距为Θ(N²)。

Conclusion: 交互测试可带来二次方的查询数量减少，但并不会产生指数级的查询优势——表面上的指数级分支并不意味着指数级效率提升。这一结论为模型评估协议的设计提供了理论指引：在预算受限时，非自适应（预先规划）方案在适度额外成本下即可达到自适应方案的性能。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [174] [BaKron: Efficient Quantization with Kronecker-Factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06291)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BaKron，一种高效的神经网络量化求解器，利用Kronecker分解的Hessian近似（双侧信息）进行自适应舍入。通过反对角线并行和递归分治构造，BaKron将总计算量从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))，顺序步数为O(m+n)，匹配GPTQ的三次缩放但利用更丰富的曲率信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPTQ风格的自适应舍入通常只使用输入激活的单侧信息，而双侧Kronecker因式Hessian近似能够捕获输出坐标间的相关性，但直接在向量化权重域应用GPTQ计算代价过高。需要一个既能利用双侧曲率信息又保持计算高效的方法。

Method: BaKron结合反对角线并行（anti-diagonal parallelism）与递归分治构造（recursive divide-and-conquer construction）。该方法建立在BoA和YAQA的双侧自适应舍入公式之上，并对基础量化器和Hessian估计器均具有模块化特性。对于m×n的权重矩阵，BaKron使用O(m+n)个顺序步骤，将总工作量从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))。

Result: BaKron在计算复杂度上匹配GPTQ的三次缩放（cubic scaling），同时利用更丰富的曲率信息。论文提供了实际基准测试，考察了多种可供BaKron使用的Hessian估计方法，找到了一种计算这些Hessian的高效技术，并进行了实验评估。

Conclusion: BaKron是一种模块化、计算高效的神经网络量化自适应舍入求解器，在保持与GPTQ相当的复杂度下，通过利用双侧Kronecker因式Hessian信息获得了更好的量化性能，并且可以灵活适配不同的基础量化器和Hessian估计器。

Abstract: We accelerate a family of algorithms for neural network quantization whose geometry is informed by any Kronecker-factored approximation of the Hessian. GPTQ-style adaptive rounding typically uses one-sided information derived from input activations. Two-sided Kronecker-factored Hessian approximations can additionally capture correlations across output coordinates, but applying GPTQ directly in the vectorized weight domain is computationally expensive. Building on the two-sided adaptive-rounding formulation used by BoA and YAQA, we introduce BaKron, an efficient solver that combines anti-diagonal parallelism with a recursive divide-and-conquer construction. For an $m\times n$ weight matrix, BaKron uses $O(m+n)$ sequential steps while reducing the total work from $O(m^2n^2)$ to $O(mn(m+n))$. Thus, it matches the cubic scaling of GPTQ while exploiting richer curvature information. Moreover, BaKron is modular with respect to both the base quantizer and the Hessian estimator. We also provide practical benchmarks, consider a range of Hessians that BaKron can be called with, find an efficient technique to compute these Hessians, and evaluate the algorithm experimentally.

</details>


### [175] [On-Policy Self-Distillation without Any Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06296)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督在线策略自蒸馏（U-OPSD）方法，利用模型自身生成数据通过内部一致性（多数投票构建伪解）实现真正的"自"蒸馏，无需任何外部监督信号（如真实标签、环境反馈或更大模型指导）。U-OPSD在各种基准、基座模型与训练设置下一致提升基础模型性能，并在多数场景下达到或超越使用真实标签的监督方法（如OPSD和GRPO）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略（自）蒸馏（OPD/OPSD）方法虽在LLM后训练中表现出潜力，但严重依赖外部监督信号（真实标签、环境反馈或更大模型引导），未能实现真正的"自"蒸馏。作者希望仅使用模型自身生成的数据即可完成蒸馏过程，消除对外部监督的依赖。

Method: U-OPSD首先采样多个rollout，在自一致性阈值下通过多数投票构建伪解；然后以最短伪解为条件构造教师分布，并将其蒸馏到模型最长错误完成的前缀中，使模型能精确地在"自信但错误"的位置进行自我纠正。整个过程仅利用模型自身的生成内容和内部一致性信号，不依赖任何外部监督。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25、MATH500和AMC23基准上，U-OPSD在Qwen3非思考模式4B和8B规模上分别比基础模型提升8.5%和10.7%，并平均超越OPSD 3.2%和2.3%。在思考模式下，4B规模超越OPSD 0.9%，8B规模与OPSD持平，同时分别超越GRPO 0.7%和1.1%。

Conclusion: U-OPSD证明仅利用模型自身生成和内部一致性即可实现有效的在线策略自蒸馏，在与依赖真实标签的监督方法（OPSD、GRPO）相比时具有竞争力甚至更优的表现，为无监督大模型后训练提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: On-policy (Self-)Distillation (OPD / OPSD) has shown strong potential for post-training large language models (LLMs). However, existing methods still rely heavily on external supervision, including ground-truth signals, environmental feedback, or guidance from larger models, and therefore fall short of genuine "self"-distillation. In this study, we show that on-policy self-distillation can be achieved using only a model's own generations via internal consistency. We propose Unsupervised On-Policy Self-Distillation (U-OPSD). U-OPSD first samples multiple rollouts and constructs a pseudo-solution by majority vote under a self-consistency threshold. It then conditions a teacher distribution on the shortest pseudo-solution and distills it into prefixes of the model's longest incorrect completion, allowing the model to correct itself precisely where it is confidently wrong. Across diverse benchmarks, base models, and training settings, U-OPSD consistently improves over the base models and matches or surpasses supervised methods with ground truth (GT), such as OPSD and GRPO. On AIME24, AIME25, HMMT25, MATH500, and AMC23, U-OPSD improves over the base model by 8.5% and 10.7% on Qwen3 non-thinking mode at the 4B and 8B scales, respectively, and outperforms OPSD by an average of 3.2% and 2.3%. In thinking mode, U-OPSD remains on par with OPSD, outperforming it by 0.9% at 4B and matching it at 8B, while surpassing GRPO by 0.7% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [176] [RRC: Unlocking Generative Reward Models in LLM Reinforcement Learning via Ranking-Based Reward Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06310)
*Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Yifu Huo,Bei Li,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Xiaoyang Hao,Yuxin Gao,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Tongran Liu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RRC（Ranking-based Reward Construction）的排序奖励构造方法，解决了生成式奖励模型（Generative Reward Model）与现有强化学习算法之间因"比较式排序"与"标量评分"范式不匹配导致的RL训练效果受限问题。RRC通过自竞争排序和锚点引导排序两种互补策略，从相对偏好排序中推导出更有效的RL学习信号，在开放对话和推理基准上显著提升训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来奖励建模从判别式转向生成式，生成式奖励模型虽然具备强大的响应排序能力，但在强化学习（RL）中未能充分发挥潜力。作者发现根本原因在于生成式奖励模型的比较式建模本质与现有RL算法采用的标量评分范式之间存在不匹配，因此需要一种桥接该差距的方案。

Method: 提出Ranking-based Reward Construction（RRC）方法：1）自竞争排序（self-competitive ranking）：利用模型在采样响应之间的相对比较来构造奖励；2）锚点引导排序（anchor-guided ranking）：通过少量参考响应实现可扩展的排序奖励构造。两种策略互补，共同将相对偏好排序转化为更有效的RL信号。

Result: 在开放式对话和推理基准上的实验表明，RRC显著改善了生成式奖励模型的RL训练效果，相较于现有奖励构造方法获得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RRC验证了基于排序构造奖励的有效性，为生成式奖励模型在RL中的实际应用提供了可行的解决方案，并已开源代码。

Abstract: Recent advances in reward modeling show a paradigm shift from discriminative reward models to generative reward models. However, despite their strong capabilities in response ranking, generative reward models have not realized their potential in reinforcement learning (RL). Our analysis reveals that this limitation arises from a mismatch between the comparative nature of generative reward modeling and the scalar scoring paradigm adopted by existing RL algorithms. To bridge this gap, we propose a Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC) approach, which enables generative reward models to provide more effective RL learning signals by deriving rewards from relative preference rankings. RRC introduces two complementary strategies: self-competitive ranking, which exploits comparisons among sampled responses, and anchor-guided ranking, which enables scalable ranking-based reward construction with a small set of reference responses. Experiments across open-ended chat and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RRC substantially improves RL training with generative reward models, achieving consistent gains over existing reward construction approaches. Our code can be found at https://github.com/wangclnlp/RRC.

</details>


### [177] [CalibForge: Adversarial Solver Calibration for Scaling Learnable Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06352)
*Fanzhe Meng,Guoxin Chen,Jiale Zhao,Shuang Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CalibForge，一个自主终端任务合成系统，通过求解器校准（多求解器校准和对比求解器校准）将候选任务修订到"可学习区间"，构建了5,431个校准后的终端任务。在该数据集上训练的模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到32.58%和47.57%，相对基线模型最高提升分别为Terminal-Bench 2.0上24.71个百分点、SWE-bench Pro上27.68个百分点和Doc2Repo上30.04个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练终端智能体需要可执行且可验证的任务，但仅仅可执行并不能保证任务对给定求解器设置具有合适的挑战难度。需要一种能够根据求解器实际行为来校准任务难度的方法，使任务落在"可学习区间"内，而非简单可解或过难。

Method: CalibForge系统包含两种校准策略：(1) 多求解器校准，针对异构求解器池内的不一致性进行修订；(2) 对比求解器校准，针对指定的强过/弱败关系进行修订。两者都将求解器可证明的求解能力作为锚点，从而构建求解器相对的可学习区间，将候选任务修订到该区间内。

Result: 构建了5,431个校准后的终端任务。消融实验表明两种校准策略都比仅人工编写加验证或仅单一求解器反馈产生更有效的监督信号。在完整数据集上训练的模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0上取得32.58%和47.57%的成绩。最大提升：Terminal-Bench 2.0上24.71个百分点、SWE-bench Pro上27.68个百分点、Doc2Repo上30.04个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器相对的可学习性（solver-relative learnability）是构建有效且可迁移的智能体训练数据的实际可行目标，CalibForge证明了通过求解器校准生成高质量任务数据的有效性。

Abstract: Training terminal agents requires executable and verifiable tasks that are not merely solvable, but appropriately challenging for learning. Executable validation establishes feasibility, yet does not reveal how a task behaves relative to a given solver setting. In this paper, we present CalibForge, an autonomous terminal-task synthesis system that uses verified solver behavior to revise candidate tasks through adversarial solver calibration. Multi-solver calibration targets disagreement within a heterogeneous solver pool, whereas contrastive solver calibration targets a designated strong-pass/weak-fail relation; both operationalize a solver-relative learnable zone anchored in demonstrated solvability. Using CalibForge, we construct 5,431 calibrated terminal tasks. Our ablations show that both strategies yield more effective supervision than authoring and validation alone or ordinary single-solver feedback. Models trained on the full collection achieve 32.58% and 47.57% on Terminal-Bench 2.0. The largest improvements over the corresponding base model reach 24.71 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.0, 27.68 points on SWE-bench Pro, and 30.04 points on Doc2Repo. Together, these results support solver-relative learnability as a practical target for constructing effective and transferable agent training data.

</details>


### [178] [An Optimal Agnostic PAC Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06363)
*Markus Engelund Mathiasen,Jian Qian,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个学习器，在有限VC维假设类上实现了二元风险的最优统计界，首次在任意固定L*下将样本复杂度与通用常数完全匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，agnostic PAC学习的样本复杂度上界与已知下界之间存在常数差距，作者致力于消除这一差距，在任意固定最优风险L*下实现真正统计意义上的最优风险界。

Method: 针对VC维为d的有限类H⊆{-1,+1}^X，构造一个特定的学习算法，从n个i.i.d.样本中输出假设h^，其风险界同时包含√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n)和(d+log(1/δ))/n两个项，常数约为7×10^8。

Result: 在概率至少1-δ下，学习器输出的风险满足L(h^) ≤ L* + 7×10^8(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)，与Devroye, Györfi和Lugosi给出的下界在通用常数意义上匹配。

Conclusion: 该项工作解决了agnostic PAC学习在有限VC维情形下的样本复杂度问题，达到了任意固定L*下最优的统计风险界，彻底弥合了上下界之间的常数差距。

Abstract: Let $H\subseteq\{-1,+1\}^X$ be a class of finite VC dimension $d\ge1$. Writing $L$ for the binary risk and $L^*=\min_{h\in H}L(h)$, we construct a learner achieving the statistically optimal risk bound: from an i.i.d.\ sample of size $n$, for every $0<δ\le 1/2$, with probability at least $1-δ$, \[
  L(\widehat h)
  \le L^*+ 7\cdot10^8\left(
  \sqrt{\frac{L^*(d+\log(1/δ))}{n}}
  +\frac{d+\log(1/δ)}{n}
  \right). \] This settles the sample complexity of agnostic PAC learning up to universal constants at every fixed $L^*$, matching the lower bounds of Devroye, Györfi, and Lugosi [A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition, Springer, 1996].

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Quality Diversity for Reliable Data Driven Time-Use Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05230)
*Aneta Neumann,Ty Stanford,Dorothea Dumuid,Frank Neumann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将预测不确定性嵌入质量多样性（QD）优化的时间使用推荐框架，利用成分数据分析从大型儿童队列数据集中建模多健康指标与日常活动组成的关系，在优化时间分配时同时考虑预期健康收益和模型置信度，从而生成更可靠的时间使用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间使用优化方法仅关注最大化预期健康收益，忽略了数据驱动预测中固有的不确定性，这可能导致不切实际且不可靠的健康相关时间分配建议。

Method: 基于大型儿童队列数据集（n>1000），使用成分数据分析（Compositional Data Analysis）构建目标函数，刻画日常活动组成与多个健康指标之间的关系；提出将预测不确定性嵌入质量多样性（QD）优化过程的新方法，通过基于变量和基于目标的两种行为表示探索解空间，在优化中权衡预期健康收益与模型置信度。

Result: 该框架能够探索多样化的高质量时间使用组成，揭示不确定性下健康结果之间的显式关系，并将推荐向低不确定性区域偏移，同时保留高质量的结构特征。

Conclusion: 将不确定性直接嵌入优化过程，可以使时间使用推荐在保持高质量结构的同时向更可靠的决策区域移动，为行为健康领域提供更可靠的时间分配建议。

Abstract: The daily allocation of the finite 24-hour time budget is strongly associated with physical, mental, and cognitive health. While predictive models can estimate the relationship between time-use compositions and health outcomes such as body mass index, life satisfaction, and cognition, most optimization approaches focus only on maximizing expected benefit and do not consider the uncertainty inherent in data-driven prediction. Ignoring uncertainty in health-related decisions can lead to unrealistic time-use recommendations. To address this gap, we introduce an uncertainty quantification Quality Diversity (QD) framework for a more reliable time-use recommendation. Objective functions are derived using compositional data analysis using a large child cohort dataset n > 1000, to capture the relationship between daily activity compositions and multiple health indicators. We develop a new approach that incorporates predictive uncertainty into QD processes and produces more reliable recommendations that balance the expected health benefits with the confidence of the model. We explore the solution space through variable-based and objective-based behavioral representations, revealing diverse high-quality time-use composition and explicit relationships between health outcomes under uncertainty. By embedding uncertainty directly into optimization, our framework shifts the time-use recommendations toward regions of lower uncertainty while preserving high-quality structures for more reliable decision-making in behavioral health.

</details>


### [180] [A Unified Causal Inference Framework for the Desirability of Outcome Ranking Paradigm in Benefit-Risk Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05244)
*Yuan Feng,Shiyu Shu,Yixin Fang,Ionut Bebu,Toshimitsu Hamasaki,Scott Evans,Guoqing Diao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的协变量校正因果推断框架，用于估计结局排序期望（DOOR）概率以进行获益-风险评估。该框架将DOOR概率表达为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的雙線性泛函，通过顺序风险集风险估计条件有序分布，并推导出DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）。模拟研究表明TMLE-SL点估计性能最佳，CVTMLE-SL在整体推断性能上最优，并以抗菌耐药性领导小组的多药耐药微生物网络数据进行了实证演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机试验和观察性研究中，进行获益-风险评估（benefit-risk evaluation）需要同时考虑疗效与安全性两个维度，而传统的DOOR概率估计方法缺乏统一的协变量校正框架，无法在存在混杂时获得无偏且高效的估计量。

Method: 将DOOR概率表示为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的雙線性泛函；通过顺序风险集危害（sequential risk-set hazards）估计条件有序分布；推导DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）；在点估计中比较G-computation、归一化IPW、AIPW与TMLE（使用GLM或Super Learner估计扰动函数）；在推断中评估AIPW-SL与TMLE-SL在有无交叉拟合下的性能。

Result: TMLE-SL在点估计中表现最强且最稳定，AIPW-SL位居第二。在EIF推断评估中（考虑重叠程度、治疗效果异质性和分配比例等设定），CVTMLE-SL在DOOR尺度偏差、有序分布恢复、标准误准确性和置信区间覆盖率方面整体表现最优。

Conclusion: 统一协变量校正框架配合交叉拟合的TMLE-SL（CVTMLE-SL）是估计DOOR概率进行获益-风险评估的推荐方法，并在抗菌耐药性领导组多药耐药微生物数据中得到了实际应用验证。

Abstract: We developed a unified covariate-adjusted causal inference framework for estimating the desirability of outcome ranking (DOOR) probability for benefit-risk evaluation in randomized trials and observational studies. The framework expresses the DOOR probability as a bilinear functional of the marginal ordinal outcome distributions under the two treatment strategies, estimates conditional ordinal distributions through sequential risk-set hazards, and derives the efficient influence function (EIF) of the DOOR probability. The point-estimation simulations compared G-computation, normalized inverse probability weighting (IPW), augmented IPW (AIPW), and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE), with nuisance functions estimated using generalized linear models or Super Learner (SL). TMLE-SL showed the strongest and most consistent point-estimation performance, with AIPW-SL ranking second. EIF-based inference was then evaluated for AIPW-SL and TMLE-SL, with and without cross-fitting, across settings varying in overlap, treatment-effect heterogeneity, and treatment allocation. CVTMLE-SL showed the strongest overall performance across DOOR-scale bias, recovery of the underlying ordinal distributions, standard-error accuracy, and confidence-interval coverage. We illustrate the methodology using data from the multidrug-resistant organism network of the Antibacterial Resistance Leadership Group.

</details>


### [181] [Deep Generalised Mixed Models: a Novel Neural Network Structure for Analysing Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05930)
*Nina van Gerwen,Dimitris Rizopoulos,Manon Hillegers,Loes Keijsers,Sten Willemsen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为深度广义混合模型（Deep Generalised Mixed Model）的新型神经网络架构，用于分析体验抽样法（ESM）收集的纵向数据，特别是针对COVID-19疫情期间青少年情绪研究的GrowIt!应用数据。该模型将传统混合效应模型推广至深度学习框架，结合变分自编码器和贝叶斯数据增强算法，能够处理高维、随机缺失的纵向数据，并适用于多种分布类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准统计技术难以扩展到高维ESM数据场景，而机器学习方法因忽略缺失数据机制（尤其是缺失随机的类型）会产生有偏结果。在GrowIt!研究中，青少年因经历强烈负面情绪而退出，导致数据并非完全随机缺失，现有方法无法对此有效处理。

Method: 提出一种将混合效应模型推广到深度学习的神经网络架构，通过固定效应和随机效应对数据的均值结构和相关结构进行半参数化、灵活建模。估计方法采用变分自编码器（VAE）的适应形式以及贝叶斯数据增强算法，使模型能够处理服从一般分布的纵向结局、扩展至高维设置，并在数据缺失随机的条件下提供有效推断。

Result: 将所提出的深度广义混合模型应用于GrowIt!研究和多种模拟实验。结果表明该模型展现出应用潜力，但由于模型不稳定性问题，性能表现欠佳。

Conclusion: 深度广义混合模型为高维、非随机缺失的纵向ESM数据提供了新颖的分析框架，概念上具有可行性，但目前仍存在模型不稳定性的挑战，需要进一步改进以提升实际应用表现。

Abstract: The experience sampling method (ESM) is a longitudinal research design where participants report their thoughts, emotional states and behaviours multiple times a day. Our work is motivated by such data collected by the GrowIt! app, which was released to investigate daily emotions among adolescents during the COVID-19 pandemic. Current procedures to analyse ESM data face various challenges. While standard statistical techniques may not scale well to a high-dimensional setting, machine learning procedures can give biased results due to selection bias introduced by missingness. In our motivating dataset, adolescents dropped out due to previous strong feelings of negative emotions. Hence, the implied missing data are of the missing-at-random type that standard machine learning procedures cannot accommodate. We develop a novel neural network architecture that generalises mixed effects models to deep learning to overcome these challenges. It allows semi-parametric and flexible modelling of data's mean and correlation structure through fixed and random effects. For estimation, we use an adaptation of variational auto-encoders and a Bayesian data augmentation algorithm. Through this approach, the model can accommodate longitudinal outcomes following generic distributions, scale well to high-dimensional settings and provide valid inference when data are missing-at-random. We applied the Deep Generalised Mixed Model to the GrowIt! study and various simulations. The results show potential for the Deep Generalised Mixed Model, yet suboptimal performance due to model instability.

</details>


### [182] [Handling Missing Data in Probabilistic Regression Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06195)
*Taiane Schaedler Prass,Alisson Silva Neimaier,Guilherme Pumi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文扩展了概率回归树（PRTrees）框架，使其能够在树构建过程中直接处理缺失预测变量，无需事先插补。提出了三种处理缺失值的策略，并在多个真实数据集上与经典回归树（CART）进行了比较，结果表明提出的方法在缺失比例较高的数据集中经常优于CART。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典回归树在处理缺失预测变量时需要事先插补，而概率回归树（PRTrees）作为一种平滑且一致的回归树替代方案，尚未具备直接处理缺失值的能力。本文旨在扩展PRTree框架，使其在树构建阶段就能自然处理任意模式下的缺失协变量值，从而消除对预插补步骤的依赖。

Method: 提出了三种利用不同可用信息的缺失值处理策略：（1）均匀概率方法（uniform-probability），（2）部分观测方法（partial-observation），（3）降维平滑方法（dimension-reduced smoothing）。这些修改被设计为在任意缺失模式下保持原始方法的基本概率性质，包括概率守恒（probability conservation）和边际兼容性（marginal compatibility）。

Result: 在多个具有不同缺失程度的数据集上，与经典回归树（CART）对比的结果表明：概率树构建的有效性高度依赖于缺失观测的处理方式。填充策略（fill strategy）是主导建模组件，其影响往往大于平滑分布或代理选择准则。在缺失预测变量比例较大的数据集中，所提方法频繁优于CART，同时保持了树模型的解释性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 扩展后的PRTree框架能够在无预插补的情况下直接处理缺失预测变量，且在缺失率较高的场景下具有优于经典CART的预测性能，同时保留了树模型的优势。填充策略是影响性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Probabilistic Regression Trees (PRTrees) are a smooth and consistent alternative to classical regression trees, producing continuous predictions through probabilistic split assignments. This paper extends the PRTree framework to accommodate missing predictor values directly during tree construction, eliminating the need for prior imputation. Three strategies are proposed, each exploiting the available information differently: a uniform-probability approach, a partial-observation approach, and a dimension-reduced smoothing approach. These modifications are defined to preserve the fundamental probabilistic properties of the original methodology, including probability conservation and marginal compatibility, under arbitrary patterns of missing covariate values. The proposed methods are evaluated on several real-world datasets exhibiting different levels of missingness and are compared with classical regression trees. The results show that the effectiveness of probabilistic tree construction depends strongly on the treatment of missing observations. Across the considered datasets, the fill strategy emerged as the dominant modeling component, often exerting a larger influence on predictive performance than either the smoothing distribution or the proxy-selection criterion. In datasets where a substantial proportion of observations contained missing predictor values, the proposed methods frequently outperformed CART, while maintaining the interpretability and flexibility of tree-based models.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond Marginal Validity: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06206)
*Anton Conrad,Rustam Isaev,Denis Belomestny,Eric Moulines,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对随机局部化共形预测（RLCP）方法，首次提供了在已实现局部化邻域上联合控制条件覆盖有效性和预言机效率的有限样本高概率界。所导出的界分解为O(h^β)的局部化偏差项和随校准规模递减的校准项，清晰地刻画了带宽的偏差-方差权衡。文章还分析了数据分裂式学习的分数（如共形化分位数回归中的枢轴分数），表明改进的学习能够锐化局部化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测虽然提供了有限样本、分布自由的边际覆盖保证，但边际有效性可能掩盖严重的协变量特定误校准，而精确的分布自由条件覆盖在有限样本下不可实现。随机局部化共形预测（RLCP）通过在测试点附近校准来缓解这一差距并保持边际覆盖，但现有理论缺乏对已实现局部化集合的有限样本保证，无法联合控制条件有效性和预言机效率。

Method: 在任意固定分数下，假设条件分数CDF满足Hölder正则性以及标准的密度与核假设，证明在已实现局部化邻域上均匀成立的高概率界，分别对条件覆盖缺口和相对预言机的长度误差进行约束。该界分解为O(h^β)的局部化偏差项和随校准规模递减的校准项。此外，对数据分裂式学习的分数进行分析，当分数目标为枢轴分数（如共形化分位数回归）时，均匀局部保证可分解为固定分数的校准误差和均匀分数估计误差。

Result: 得到了联合控制条件覆盖缺口和预言机长度误差的高概率界，揭示了带宽的偏差-方差权衡，明确了RLCP在何种条件下能够追踪预言机。对于学习得到的分数，证明了改进了的分数学习能够锐化局部化保证，因为保证可分解为固定分数校准与分数估计误差两部分。

Conclusion: 本文为RLCP提供了首个统一的有限样本理论框架，将条件有效性与预言机效率以可分解的偏差-校准形式刻画，为带宽选择和学习分数的设计提供了理论依据，填补了RLCP在已实现局部化保证方面的理论空白。

Abstract: Conformal prediction endows arbitrary black-box predictors with finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, yet marginal validity can hide severe covariate-specific miscalibration, while exact distribution-free conditional coverage is finite-sample unattainable. Randomly localized conformal prediction (RLCP) mitigates this gap by calibrating near the test point while preserving marginal coverage. Existing theory, however, lacks finite-sample guarantees for the realized localized set that jointly control conditional validity and oracle efficiency. We provide such guarantees. For any fixed score, under Hölder regularity of the conditional score CDF and standard density and kernel assumptions, we prove high-probability bounds, uniform over a realized localization neighbourhood, for the conditional-coverage gap and the length error relative to the oracle. The bounds decompose into an $O(h^β)$ localization bias and a calibration term decreasing with calibration size, clarifying the bandwidth bias-variance tradeoff and when RLCP tracks the oracle. We also analyze data-split learned scores: when the score targets a pivotal score, as in conformalized quantile regression, uniform local guarantees decompose into fixed-score calibration and uniform score-estimation errors, showing that improved learning sharpens localized guarantees.

</details>


### [184] [Minimax Optimal Early-Stopped Gradient Descent for Gaussian Mixture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06250)
*Alex Buna,Shirley Xiaoqi Liu,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究过参数化分类中梯度下降（GD）的隐式偏差导致的统计次优性问题，证明在带标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下，适当时机早停的GD能达到极小极大最优的excess zero-one风险，并首次去除了标准bound中的平方根速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化分类中训练数据可分但底层分布不可分时，GD在logistic loss上会在范数上发散、方向上收敛到最大间隔插值分类器，其隐式偏差可能统计次优。作者希望证明早停能克服这种次优性，实现统计最优性能。

Method: 理论分析分为三部分：(1) 对早停迭代给出尖锐的上界；(2) 对任意分类器给出匹配的统计下界，由此得到最优速率；(3) 核心技术创新是新的校准（calibration）结果，将excess logistic risk转化为excess zero-one risk，该结果处理了标签翻转噪声导致的模型误设，并移除了标准bound中的平方根速率。此外，还建立了线性插值器的下界，说明插值方法达到同等excess risk需要指数更多的样本。

Result: 在具有多项式与指数谱衰减（快速且连续衰减的协方差谱）的高斯混合模型下，早停的GD在适当的oracle时刻达到极小极大最优excess zero-one风险；插值器（最大间隔分类器）则需要指数更多的样本才能达到相同excess risk。实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 早停是克服过参数化GD隐式偏差次优性的有效策略；新的校准工具为模型误设下的风险转化提供了更紧的界，具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: In overparameterised classification, training data can be linearly separable even when the underlying distribution is not. In this setting, gradient descent (GD) on the logistic loss diverges in norm while converging in direction to a max-margin interpolating classifier, whose implicit bias can be statistically suboptimal. In this work, we show that early stopping can overcome this suboptimality: in a Gaussian mixture model with label-flipping noise, GD stopped at an appropriate oracle time achieves minimax-optimal excess zero-one risk for covariance spectra with fast and continuous decay, including polynomial and exponential spectral decays. Our analysis combines a sharp upper bound for the early-stopped iterate with a matching statistical lower bound over arbitrary classifiers, yielding optimal rates that are validated by experiments. A central technical contribution is a new calibration result that converts excess logistic risk into excess zero-one risk; it handles the model misspecification induced by the label-flipping noise, and removes the square-root rate in standard bounds. We also establish a lower bound for linear interpolators, showing that interpolation can require exponentially more samples than early stopping to achieve the same excess risk.

</details>


### [185] [Stochastic Dynamics on Persistence Diagram Space via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06276)
*Farzana Nasrin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在持续同调图（PD）空间上的强化学习框架，用于建模拓扑结构的随机演化动力学。通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作定义受控马尔可夫过程，建立了马尔可夫链的不可约性、周期性、几何遍历性条件，从而保证PD空间上唯一稳态概率分布的存在。同时设计包含分布匹配、任务特定拓扑统计和结构保持压缩的多目标奖励函数，实现自适应拓扑简化与概率建模。在合成数据和神经影像PD上验证了该方法能在降低复杂度时保留主要拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久性图（PD）的统计分析大多将图视为静态对象，缺乏在PD空间上进行概率建模和随机演化的框架。为了支持动态拓扑分析、拓扑数据生成和概率驱动的研究场景，需要一种能够描述PD随机演化的受控动力学模型。

Method: 提出强化学习框架，在PD空间上定义拓扑感知的局部编辑操作（如点增删、移动等），从而构造受控马尔可夫过程。针对变基数PD空间，建立马尔可夫链不可约性、非周期性与几何遍历性的条件，确保唯一稳态分布存在。奖励函数由三部分组成：分布匹配奖励、任务特定拓扑统计奖励、以及结构保持压缩奖励，综合平衡分布目标、图保真度和复杂度缩减。

Result: 在合成数据和神经影像PD上的实验表明，所提框架能够在显著降低图复杂度（点数量）的同时，保留主导的拓扑结构（如主要同调生成元），验证了方法的有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: 本文首次将强化学习与PD空间上的随机动力学结合，提供了可计算的自适应拓扑简化与概率建模框架，拓展了持续同调从静态统计到动态演化的分析范式。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams (PDs) provide stable and interpretable summaries of multiscale topological structure. While substantial progress has been made in the statistical analysis of PDs, existing literature often treats diagrams as static objects and provide limited frameworks for probabilistic modeling and stochastic evolution on PD space. We introduce a reinforcement learning framework for stochastic dynamics on PD space, where diagrams evolve through topology aware local edit operations. The dynamics define controlled Markov processes on spaces of finite PDs with variable cardinality. We establish conditions under which the induced Markov chains are irreducible, aperiodic, and geometrically ergodic, implying the existence of unique stationary probability laws on PD space. To guide the dynamics toward scientifically relevant topological targets, we formulate objectives that encompass distribution matching, task specific topological statistics, and structure-preserving compression. The resulting rewards balance task specific distributional targets, diagram fidelity, and complexity reduction, and yield a framework for adaptive topological simplification and probabilistic modeling. Experiments on synthetic and neuroimaging PDs demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve dominant topological structure while reducing diagram complexity.

</details>


### [186] [Optimal Rates for Learning with Monotone Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06337)
*Anay Mehrotra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明在"单调对手"（monotone adversary）插入正确标注示例的模型下，额外对数因子是固有的而非算法伪影：VC维d≥2时最大最小期望误差为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))，VC维1时为Θ(1/n)；同样的下界也适用于Littlestone维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Larsen等人引入了一个新模型：单调对手在干净样本上插入额外正确标注的示例，使组合样本不再可交换。他们证明了ERM的误差为O((d/n)log(n/d))，且所有已知最优学习器都会被推离Θ(d/n)的最优率，但留下了一个关键开放问题：这个额外对数因子是特定算法的伪影，还是缺乏可交换性的固有后果？

Method: 作者通过一个统一的构造来证明所有下界：一个显式的类别和先验分布，其中两个目标假设在非零质量点上不同，却产生相同样本。对于VC维1的情况，他们使用简单的不恰当学习器，其分析适配了单包含图（one-inclusion graph）背后的留一论证。

Result: 在VC维d≥2时，最大最小期望误差为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))；在d=1时为Θ(1/n)。同样结果对Littlestone维d_L也成立，表明干净的在线到批量（online-to-batch）率O(d_L/n)无法实现。因此，反直觉地，添加正确标注的示例会使学习变得更困难（增加对数因子），即使对具有有限错误界限的类别也是如此。

Conclusion: 额外对数因子是缺乏可交换性的固有代价，而非特定算法的伪影。该结果揭示了在分布偏移/依赖样本情况下，即使是正确标注的数据也可能对学习造成实质性困难。

Abstract: A monotone adversary observes an i.i.d. labeled sample and appends a finite number of further examples of its choice, every one of them labeled correctly by the target hypothesis. The learner sees a uniform shuffle of the combined sample and is scored on the original distribution. Every example is correctly labeled, but the insertions depend on the clean sample, so the combined sample is not exchangeable. Larsen, Pabbaraju, and Shetty, who introduced this model, showed that empirical risk minimization attains expected error $O((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for classes of VC dimension $d$, and that every known optimal learner can be pushed away from the $Θ(d/n)$ rate, optimal for PAC learning. They asked whether the extra logarithm is an artifact of those particular algorithms or an inherent consequence of the lack of exchangeability.
  We show that this additional cost is inherent beyond VC dimension one. In the worst case over classes of VC dimension $d$ and over known finite insertion budgets, the minimax expected error is $Θ(1/n)$ at $d=1$ and $Θ((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for $d\geq 2$. The same rates hold with Littlestone dimension $d_{\mathrm L}$ in place of $d$, so the clean online-to-batch rate $O(d_{\mathrm L}/n)$ is unattainable as well. Thus, somewhat counterintuitively, adding correctly labeled examples can make learning harder by a logarithmic factor, even for classes that admit finite mistake bounds in online learning. The dimension-one upper bound is achieved by a simple improper learner whose analysis adapts the leave-one-out argument underlying the one-inclusion graph. All of our lower bounds are elementary and come from a single construction: an explicit class and prior on which two target hypothesis, which differ a point of nonnegligible mass, produce the same sample.

</details>


### [187] [Scalable estimation of VARMA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06340)
*Daniel Paulin,Victor Elvira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算框架，使VARMA模型的估计在每次优化迭代中不再依赖于时间序列长度T。通过偏自相关重参数化保证平稳性和可逆性，结合高斯先验和Parseval傅里叶恒等式将损失函数只依赖于固定大小的充分统计量，从而在近线性计算成本内实现估计。该框架适用于季节性、外生变量和滚动窗口场景，且在高维（d=10到d=40）下仍能保持接近Oracle的预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VARMA模型长期以来被认为在中高维度下不实用，主要面临三大障碍：似然函数非凸、参数化仅模等价类可识别、每次似然评估都需要遍历整个序列。然而VARMA的移动平均项能以少量参数匹配纯自回归需要大量滞后才能表达的动力学。本文的目标是消除这一计算瓶颈，使VARMA估计能应用于实践中目前只能依赖VAR模型的规模。

Method: 该框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 偏自相关重参数化，从构造上保证平稳性和可逆性；(2) 高斯先验，对参数化的系数设置对角项和非对角项不同的尺度；(3) 损失函数仅通过固定大小的充分统计量依赖数据，利用Parseval（傅里叶）恒等式以截断长度的近线性成本求值。由此产生两个点估计器：正则化最小二乘拟合和协方差边缘化的最大后验估计器。

Result: 理论上证明了两个估计器在固定维度下能以近参数速率恢复真实过程的无限自回归表示，截断不引入渐近偏差。该框架以相同的领先成本扩展到季节动态、外生回归变量（VARMAX）和滚动窗口重拟合。实证上，在d=10到d=40范围内估计器与Oracle预测误差保持接近（而经典条件MLE在此场景下产生不可逆拟合，预测发散），并在零售需求、气象和空气质量数据上匹配或超越VAR、贝叶斯VAR、分量级ARMA和稀疏VARMA基线。

Conclusion: 这一工作使得基于似然的VARMA估计得以在序列长度无关的单次迭代成本下应用于实践者此前只能依赖VAR模型的问题规模，显著扩展了VARMA模型的实用范围。

Abstract: Vector autoregressive moving-average (VARMA) models have long been considered impractical beyond moderate dimensions: the likelihood is non-convex, the parametrization is identified only up to equivalence, and every evaluation costs a pass over the entire series. Yet their moving-average term captures with a few parameters what a pure autoregression matches only with many lags. We introduce an estimation framework that removes this computational barrier: each optimization iteration is independent of the series length $T$. The framework combines a partial-autocorrelation reparametrization that guarantees stationarity and invertibility by construction, Gaussian priors on the reparametrized coefficients with separate scales for diagonal and off-diagonal entries, and losses that depend on the data only through fixed-size sufficient statistics, evaluated by a Parseval (Fourier) identity at near-linear cost in the truncation length. This yields two point estimators: a regularized least-squares fit and a covariance-marginalized maximum-a-posteriori estimator. We prove that both recover the infinite-autoregressive representation of the true process at a near-parametric rate in fixed dimension, so the truncation introduces no asymptotic bias. The same machinery extends, at the same leading cost, to seasonal dynamics, exogenous regressors (VARMAX), and rolling-window refits. Empirically, the estimators stay close to the oracle forecast error from $d=10$ to $d=40$ (where classical conditional MLE returns non-invertible fits whose forecasts diverge) and match or beat VAR, Bayesian-VAR, component-wise ARMA, and sparse-VARMA baselines on retail-demand, meteorological, and air-quality data. This brings likelihood-based VARMA estimation, at a per-iteration cost independent of the series length, to the problem sizes where practitioners have so far relied on VAR models.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Contour integral methods and model order reduction for parametric linear control systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05363)
*Serkan Gugercin,Mattia Manucci*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轮廓积分法（CIM）的框架，用于高效计算参数化线性控制系统在指定时间区间内的输出，并达到用户指定的精度。该方法通过修改积分轮廓上的求积规则来近似逆拉普拉斯变换，并与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）方法有效结合，采用贪心算法构建投影空间，同时自然地满足Hermite插值条件，从而大幅降低了参数域内输入-输出关系评估的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化线性控制系统的输入-输出关系计算在广泛参数域内通常是计算密集型的，现有的投影式MOR方法在精度和效率之间难以取得良好平衡，需要一种既能保证用户指定精度又能显著降低计算成本的方法。

Method: 1) 提出改进的轮廓积分法（CIM），沿修改的积分轮廓施加求积规则来近似逆拉普拉斯变换；2) 将CIM与投影式模型降阶（MOR）相结合，通过贪心算法构建投影空间；3) 推导出该设置下的误差估计以指导贪心算法；4) 证明该投影框架自然满足Hermite插值条件，增强近似精度。

Result: 在基准的非参数化和参数化控制系统上验证了方法的准确性和效率，并与最先进的投影式MOR方法进行了比较，结果表明该方法在广泛输入函数和可由低维子空间良好捕获的初始条件下具有显著的成本优势。

Conclusion: CIM与投影式MOR的结合为参数化线性系统的高效输出计算提供了一种可靠且精确的解决方案，其Hermite插值特性进一步提升了性能，展示了超越现有最先进方法的潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces a contour integral method (CIM) for efficiently computing outputs of parametric linear systems in control form over specified time intervals and to a user-prescribed accuracy. The CIM approximates the inverse Laplace transform via a quadrature rule applied along a modified integration contour. For parametric systems, we show how CIM integrates effectively with projection-based model order reduction (MOR) where a greedy algorithm builds the projection spaces following an error estimate we derive for this setting. We additionally demonstrate that the developed projection framework naturally enforces Hermite interpolation conditions. This combination substantially lowers the cost of evaluating the input-output relations across the parameter domain, for a wide range of input functions, and for initial conditions well captured by a low-dimensional subspace.. We demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the approach on benchmark non-parametric and parametric control systems, comparing against state-of-the-art projection-based MOR methods.

</details>


### [189] [An Exact Solution of the Two-Ball Multi-Look Search Game with Three Boxes and Heterogeneous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05406)
*Igor Kleiner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个异构多查找搜索成本博弈：藏匿者（Hider）将两个相同球分布到三个搜索成本不同的盒子中，搜索者（Searcher）自适应地打开盒子直到找到两个球。文章给出了该零和博弈的精确解，博弈值由三个显式有理函数的最大值给出。作者识别出72种非浪费确定性搜索策略（等价于支付收益仅42种），并用精确有理数证明、Bernstein基证书以及独立实现验证了整个参数区域的混合策略最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题的核心难点在于搜索成本异构（a≥b≥c>0）且目标包含两个球，使得搜索策略空间和最优策略分析远比均匀成本或单目标情形复杂。已有工作未给出该一般情形的精确解，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 文章将博弈建模为零和线性规划问题。识别出所有非浪费确定性搜索策略等价于72棵基本决策树（仅对应42个不同的开箱次数分布）。然后分三个参数区域：第一区域通过解析论证求解；第二区域用两个精确的搜索者混合策略求解；第三区域用四个混合策略求解。混合策略在整个成本区域上的可行性通过精确有理Bernstein基证书来验证，并由独立实现复现策略集、零和线性规划值及154个二进制节点验证。

Result: 博弈值等于三个显式有理函数的最大值。在三个参数区域中，最优藏匿策略分别是限制在3、5或6种放置上的乘积分布。文章通过精确有理数计算和独立验证复现了完整策略集、所有线性规划值以及154个Bernstein验证节点。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了三盒双球异构搜索成本博弈，给出了博弈值、最优藏匿策略和最优混合搜索策略的精确刻画，并通过精确有理证书和独立实现双重验证了结果的正确性。

Abstract: A Hider distributes two identical balls among three boxes whose search costs satisfy $a\ge b\ge c>0$. A Searcher opens boxes adaptively until both balls are found; every opening incurs the corresponding box cost and recovers at most one ball. We give an exact solution of this heterogeneous multi-look search-cost game. The value is the maximum of three explicit rational functions. In the three parameter regimes, an optimal Hider strategy is the product-form distribution restricted respectively to three, five, or all six placements. Every non-wasteful deterministic Searcher policy is payoff-equivalent to one of $72$ elementary decision trees, which induce only $42$ distinct opening-count profiles. An analytic argument settles the first regime. Two exact Searcher mixtures settle the second, and four settle the third. Feasibility of the parameterized mixtures over the full cost region is established by exact rational Bernstein-basis certificates. Independent implementations reproduce the policy set, the zero-sum linear-program values, and all $154$ dyadic nodes examined by the Bernstein verifier.

</details>


### [190] [A Relaxation-Based Decomposition Approach for Solving a Supported-Evacuation Problem in Wildfires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05413)
*Shahryar Moradi,Antoine Sauré,Jonathan Patrick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段随机优化模型，用于野火期间脆弱人群（如医院患者和长期护理居民）的疏散支持，同时优化设施选址、车队规模和车辆路径（含严格时间窗约束），并采用基于逻辑的Benders分解方法解决NP难问题，通过科罗拉多州Roxborough Park的社区野火演练真实数据验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火期间脆弱人群（如住院患者和长期护理居民）的疏散支持是一个关键但研究不足的领域，需要针对性的优化方法来提高疏散效率。

Method: 建立两阶段随机优化模型，联合优化设施选址、车队规模和带严格时间窗的车辆路径；针对NP难复杂度，提出基于逻辑的Benders分解（Logic-Based Benders Decomposition）方法，结合组合Benders割（Combinatorial Benders Cuts）与基于逻辑的不等式。

Result: 通过大量数值实验以及科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区野火演练的真实数据验证，所提方法能获得高质量解，在避难所选址、车辆利用率和整体疏散效率方面显著优于其他备选策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效改善野火场景下脆弱人群疏散的设施布局与运输资源配置，提升整体疏散效率，为该领域提供了一种高效可行的求解框架。

Abstract: This study addresses the critical yet under researched area of supported evacuation for vulnerable populations during wildfires, such as hospital patients and long term care residents, by developing a two stage stochastic optimization model that optimizes facility location, fleet sizing, and vehicle routing under strict time windows. To overcome the problem NP hard complexity, the authors propose an innovative solution methodology leveraging Logic Based Benders Decomposition, featuring Combinatorial Benders Cuts and logic based inequalities. Extensive numerical experiments and real world data from a community wildfire drill in Roxborough Park, Colorado, demonstrate that the proposed approach yields high quality solutions, significantly improving shelter placement, vehicle utilization, and overall evacuation efficiency compared to alternative policies.

</details>


### [191] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化期望代价（其被积函数可能非光滑且非凸）与下半连续、prox-有界函数的和。该方法在广义的非光滑局部化下降引理假设下工作，采用逐步细化的样本平均近似和Armijo型线搜索，在样本规模仅为非降且无界（无需指定增长率）的宽松条件下，建立了函数值几乎必然收敛和驻点收敛（包括全轨迹收敛与多项式收敛速率）的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机优化方法在处理"非光滑+非凸"的期望代价与非凸正则项的组合时存在明显局限：要么要求正则项的（弱）凸性，要么需要随机预言机方差的均匀上界，且样本规模通常需要满足特定的增长率要求。本文旨在突破这些限制，提出一个能涵盖更广泛问题形式的统一框架。

Method: 提出近端随机次梯度方法：每步用逐步细化的样本平均近似期望代价，采用Armijo型线搜索来选取近端-次梯度步长，确保充分下降性质在随机误差下仍然成立。该方法基于非光滑局部化下降引理的广义假设（涵盖Lipschitz梯度的光滑损失及其与凸函数的差），样本规模只需非降且无界即可。

Result: 建立了函数值序列的几乎必然收敛性和每个聚点的驻点性；借助Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（KL）性质将子序列收敛提升为全轨迹收敛到单一驻点；对指数型KL去奇异化函数和多项式增长的样本规模，导出了函数值和迭代点的显式多项式收敛速率（至多相差对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该框架比现有方法适用于更广泛的问题形式，既不需要正则项的弱凸性，也不需要随机预言机方差的均匀上界，且在光滑情形下也提供了新的收敛保证。样本规模的非降无界条件比以往方法要求的特定增长率更为宽松。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

</details>


### [192] [An Inertial Block Proximal Linearized Method with Adaptive Momentum for Nonconvex and Nonsmooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05502)
*Weifeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类多块非凸非光滑优化问题，提出了带两阶段自适应动量的惯性块近端线性化方法（IBPL⁺-TP）。该方法引入两阶段自适应动量策略来有效更新外推参数，允许使用两个不同的外推点加速收敛，并证明该方法能保证目标函数的单调收敛、序列全局收敛到临界点且具有收敛速率。实验表明该方法在稀疏非负矩阵分解和非负CP分解等机器学习问题上优于多种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多块非凸非光滑优化问题广泛存在于地震前异常分析和机器学习等应用中，现有方法在外推参数更新、加速收敛以及参数灵活性方面存在局限，需要一种更高效且具有理论保证的求解方法。

Method: 提出惯性块近端线性化方法（IBPL⁺-TP），核心创新包括：(1) 两阶段自适应动量策略用于有效更新外推参数；(2) 允许使用两个不同的外推点以加速收敛；(3) 两个外推点的外推参数彼此独立且不受其他参数约束。

Result: 证明了目标函数的单调收敛性、序列全局收敛到临界点，并建立了收敛速率。在稀疏非负矩阵分解（ℓ₀约束）和稀疏非负CP分解（ℓ₀约束）两个非凸非光滑机器学习问题上的数值实验表明，该方法优于多种当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: IBPL⁺-TP方法在保持理论收敛保证的同时，通过两阶段自适应动量和双外推点策略显著提升了非凸非光滑多块优化问题的求解效率，在实际应用中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of multiblock nonconvex nonsmooth optimization problems, which covers many applications such as the analysis of pre-earthquake anomalies and machine learning. To solve this class of problems, we propose the inertial block proximal linearized method with two-phase adaptive momentum (IBPL$^+$-TP). Compared to the current methods, our method possesses three main advantages: (1) it introduces a two-phase adaptive momentum strategy to effectively update the extrapolation parameters, (2) it allows using two different extrapolation points to accelerate the convergence, (3) it allows the extrapolation parameters of these two extrapolation points to be independent of and unconstrained by all other parameters. While maintaining the above advantages, we prove that our method ensures the monotonic convergence of the objective function of this class of problems, and we also prove that the sequence generated by our method globally converges to a critical point, as well as establish the convergence rate of our method. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, we apply it to solve two nonconvex and nonsmooth machine learning problems, namely sparse nonnegative matrix factorization with $\ell_0$-constraints and sparse nonnegative CP decomposition with $\ell_0$-constraints. The numerical experimental results on solving these problems show that our method outperforms several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [193] [A Unified Framework for Iterate Convergence of Bregman Proximal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05536)
*He Chen,Jiaming Fan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的迭代收敛分析框架，适用于广泛的Bregman近端方法（BPM）及其核函数和复合目标函数。通过扩展缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质并引入依赖于核的参数化函数，证明了所有连续半解析函数（特别是闭域核）满足扩展SKŁ性质，从而建立了离散和连续时间下BPM的轨迹收敛理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bregman近端方法的迭代收敛问题长期悬而未决，尤其是在非凸目标下。此前工作仅对Shannon熵核和线性约束问题建立了收敛性，缺乏适用于广泛核函数和复合目标的统一收敛理论框架。

Method: 本文在chen2026skl工作的基础上扩展了SKŁ性质这一核心分析工具。具体方法包括：（1）引入依赖于核函数的参数化函数（parameterization functions）；（2）证明所有连续半解析函数在核具有闭域时满足扩展SKŁ性质；（3）验证标准BPM在温和正则条件下满足框架假设；（4）基于参数化函数进一步分析连续时间BPM（镜像流）在o-minimal可定义目标函数下的收敛行为。

Result: （1）建立了统一的迭代收敛框架，适用于广泛的核函数和复合目标函数；（2）证明了扩展SKŁ性质对连续半解析函数成立；（3）验证了标准BPM在温和条件下满足框架假设，确立了其迭代收敛性；（4）证明镜像流在o-minimal可定义目标下收敛到稳定点，这是首个不依赖凸性假设或稳定点孤立性假设的轨迹收敛结果。

Conclusion: 本文为BPM的离散和连续时间收敛提供了统一的轨迹收敛理论，显著扩大了适用范围（从特定的Shannon熵核推广到广泛的核函数类），并在连续时间情形下首次实现了无需凸性或孤立性假设的镜像流轨迹收敛证明，为后续该领域的研究提供了系统的分析框架。

Abstract: Iterate convergence of Bregman proximal methods (BPMs) has long remained open, especially for nonconvex objectives. Recently, \citet{chen2026skl} made progress by establishing iterate convergence for a BPM via the so-called scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (SKŁ) property, but only for the Shannon entropy kernel and linearly constrained problems. In this paper, we develop a unified iterate convergence framework that applies to a broad group of kernels and composite objective functions. Our approach extends the analytical tools in \cite{chen2026skl}, in particular the SKŁ property, which plays a central role in ensuring convergence of the generated sequences. By introducing kernel-dependent parameterization functions, we show that the extended SKŁ property holds for all continuous subanalytic functions, particularly when the kernel has a closed domain. We then verify that the assumptions of the framework are satisfied by standard BPMs under mild regularity conditions, thereby establishing their iterate convergence for a wide range of objective functions. Furthermore, based on the parameterization functions, we show that the continuous-time BPM (mirror flow) converges to a stationary point for o-minimal definable objective functions, yielding the first trajectory convergence result for mirror flow without imposing convexity assumptions on the objective function or isolation assumptions on stationary points. Taken together, these discrete- and continuous-time convergence results provide a unified trajectory convergence theory for BPMs.

</details>


### [194] [A Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter for Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05559)
*Lei Ma,Yuzhong Hu,Xiaoming Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出RD-YYF（重定心域Yau-Yau滤波器），通过固定大小局部窗口追踪最新状态估计中心，结合物理信息神经网络（PINN）离线生成FKE解快照与主成分分析（PCA）低维表示，在线使用轻量代理预测密度演化。实验表明其在两个几何约束目标跟踪任务中误差低于EKF和PF，且保持高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau-Yau滤波器将非线性状态估计转化为前向Kolmogorov方程（FKE）的概率密度传播问题，但在目标跟踪应用中，需要在有限计算域上高效近似FKE。现有方法在计算效率和精度上存在权衡，难以兼顾高精度密度近似与实时在线推理。

Method: 1. 提出重定心域机制：以最新状态估计为中心构建固定大小的局部窗口求解FKE，将数值分辨率集中在主导后验密度附近。2. 离线阶段：使用PINN生成FKE解快照，PCA构建密度演化的低维表示。3. 构建轻量残差代理模型，将初始条件系数与域中心映射到终端解系数。4. 在线阶段：预训练代理预测重定心窗口内的密度演化，随后进行观测更新与状态估计。

Result: 在两个几何约束的目标跟踪示例中，RD-YYF的跟踪误差低于扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）和粒子滤波（PF），同时保持高效的单时间步推理。消融实验表明，域重定心机制改善了高概率区域的密度近似精度，并加速了离线PINN的收敛。

Conclusion: RD-YYF展示了在非线性目标跟踪中实现高效且高精度估计的潜力，域重定心与离线-在线分离架构为Yau-Yau滤波器的实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The Yau-Yau filter reformulates nonlinear state estimation as probability-density propagation governed by the Forward Kolmogorov equation (FKE). Applying it to target tracking, however, requires efficient FKE approximation on a finite computational domain. This paper proposes a Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter (RD-YYF), which solves the FKE within a fixed-size local window centered at the latest state estimate. This design concentrates numerical resolution near the dominant posterior density. Offline, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) generate FKE solution snapshots, while principal component analysis constructs a low-dimensional representation of density evolution. A lightweight residual surrogate maps the initial-condition coefficients and domain center to the terminal-solution coefficients. Online, the pretrained surrogate predicts density evolution within the recentered window, followed by observation update and state estimation.
