Experiments on two geometrically constrained target-tracking examples show that RD-YYF achieves lower tracking errors than the extended Kalman filter (EKF) and particle filter (PF), while retaining efficient per-timestep inference. Ablation results indicate that domain recentering improves density approximation in high-probability regions and accelerates offline PINN convergence. These results demonstrate the potential of RD-YYF for efficient nonlinear target tracking

</details>


### [195] [SDDmiP.jl: A Software Package with a Provably Convergent Benders Algorithm for Multi-Stage Stochastic Mixed-Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05567)
*Akul Bansal,Simge Küçükyavuz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源软件包，实现了针对多阶段随机整数规划的、可证明收敛的Benders型分解算法。该算法整合了标准切割族（Benders、强化Benders、Lagrangian切割）以及ReLU切割以处理混合整数状态变量的收敛性保证。针对对偶问题多最优解的问题，提出了基于归一化和正则化的切割选择策略，并结合交替切割准则来平衡计算成本与切割质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机整数规划的计算复杂度高，现有Benders分解方法在处理混合整数状态变量时缺乏收敛性保证。虽然ReLU切割可提供收敛性，但其对偶问题常存在多个最优解，导致切割质量参差不齐。因此需要有效的切割选择策略来强化切割并提高算法实际性能。

Method: 实现一个开源的Benders型分解算法软件包，包含标准切割族（Benders、强化Benders、Lagrangian切割）和ReLU切割（保证混合整数状态变量的收敛性）。提出了两种基于对偶问题归一化和正则化的切割选择策略以挑选最优的ReLU切割，并引入交替切割准则，当Benders切割有效时使用更便宜的切割，必要时才调用更昂贵的紧致切割。在四类多阶段随机整数规划实例上进行计算实验。

Result: 在四类多阶段随机整数规划问题上进行了基准测试，实验结果揭示了问题结构对切割选择策略实际性能的影响规律。

Conclusion: 该软件包和切割选择策略为多阶段随机整数规划提供了实用且可证明收敛的求解方案，交替切割准则在计算效率与切割质量之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: We present an open-source software package that implements a provably convergent Benders-type decomposition algorithm for multistage stochastic integer programs. In addition to standard cut families, such as Benders, strengthened Benders, and Lagrangian cuts, the algorithm incorporates rectified linear unit (ReLU) cuts, which provide convergence guarantees for general mixed-integer state variables. However, the dual problems used to generate these cuts often admit multiple optimal solutions. Although each solution yields a valid cut that separates the incumbent, the resulting cuts can differ in how well they approximate the subproblem cost. To strengthen these cuts, our package implements and evaluates two cut-selection strategies based on normalization and regularization of the dual problem. We also incorporate an alternating-cut criterion that uses cheaper Benders cuts when they are effective and invokes more expensive tight cuts only when necessary. Computational experiments on four classes of multistage stochastic integer programs benchmark these methods and provide insights on how problem structure affects their practical performance.

</details>


### [196] [Convergence Rates for Variational Inequality Projection Neural Networks with a State-Dependent Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05574)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究闭凸集上变分不等式的连续时间投影神经网络，针对一种既非Hessian生成也非状态依赖标量度量的矩阵度量，证明了投影关于参数和度量的联合正则性、局部和全局指数收敛性，并发现强单调线性算子与二次连续可微一致正定度量可产生周期轨道环面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间投影神经网络的收敛性分析仅覆盖Hessian生成的逆度量（可用Bregman距离消去度量导数项）和状态依赖的标量度量，缺乏对更一般矩阵度量的理论保证，这限制了该框架在实际应用中的适用范围。

Method: 将状态相关矩阵度量引入投影动力系统，分析投影在参数和度量双重变量下的联合正则性；在常见谱界假设下改进欧氏Lipschitz估计从平方界到线性界；通过推导显式度量变化界，对Lipschitz强单调算子与Lipschitz度量证明局部与全局指数收敛，并给出显式收敛速率和半径。

Result: 证明了投影关于参数和度量的联合正则性；欧氏Lipschitz估计从平方界改进为原始界；在Lipschitz强单调算子和Lipschitz度量下获得带显式速率和半径的局部指数收敛，对紧可行集获得全局指数收敛；通过确定性计算验证了解析公式并量化了收敛证书中的松弛量。

Conclusion: 该研究表明逆度量为非Hessian可积的矩阵度量在投影神经网络中仍可保证指数收敛，且二次连续可微一致正定度量配合强单调线性算子可产生周期轨道环面，其中逆度量在整个环面上违反Hessian可积性，揭示了度量结构对动力学行为的重要影响。

Abstract: We study continuous-time projection neural networks for variational inequalities on closed convex sets. A positive definite matrix that depends on the state preconditions the operator, and its inverse defines the projection metric. Existing convergence analyses of this flow cover Hessian-generated inverse metrics and state-dependent scalar metrics. In the first case, a Bregman distance eliminates metric-derivative terms. We treat a matrix metric whose inverse is of neither kind. We prove joint regularity of the projection in its argument and metric. Under common spectral bounds, the Euclidean Lipschitz estimate improves from the squared bound to the bound itself. For Lipschitz strongly monotone operators and Lipschitz metrics, we prove local exponential convergence with explicit rate and radius. On compact feasible sets, an explicit metric-variation bound yields global exponential convergence. A twice continuously differentiable, uniformly positive definite metric and a strongly monotone linear operator produce an annulus of periodic orbits. The inverse metric violates Hessian integrability throughout the annulus. Deterministic computations confirm the analytic formulas and quantify slack in both convergence certificates.

</details>


### [197] [An inverse mixed-integer optimization framework for learning interpretable models of expert decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05585)
*Anurag Holani,Rishabh Gupta,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种逆向优化方法，通过观察专家的实际决策来联合学习其偏好（感知成本）和决策规则，从而构建可解释的模型来捕捉和复现专家的决策过程，并在三个案例研究中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家决策过程通常隐含且难以显式表达，但将其转移到训练新手、改善人机系统性能以及实现自主系统方面具有重要意义。因此需要通过专家的历史决策来学习可解释的决策模型。

Method: 采用逆向优化（inverse optimization）方法，从观察到的专家决策中联合学习两个关键组件：决策者的偏好（感知成本）和支配其选择行为的决策规则。

Result: 在三个案例研究（班次分配问题、生产规划问题和实际路线规划问题）中，同时建模感知成本和决策规则的方法取得了更好的预测效果，验证了所提出框架的灵活性和有效性。

Conclusion: 联合学习感知成本和决策规则的逆向优化框架能够更灵活地捕捉和复现专家决策过程，在复杂工程应用中具有广泛适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Understanding how experts make decisions and being able to transfer that knowledge is important, especially in complex engineering applications. It is highly valuable for training novices, improving the performance of human-machine systems, and potentially enabling fully autonomous systems that perform as well as human experts. However, an expert's decision-making strategy, developed through years of experience, is often not directly accessible, since the implicit preferences and decision rules involved can be difficult to specify explicitly. This has motivated the use of observed decisions made by the expert to learn an interpretable model that captures the expert's decision-making process. In this work, we develop an inverse optimization approach to jointly learn the decision-maker's preferences (or perceived costs) and the decision rules governing their choices. We demonstrate the general applicability of our approach using three case studies that consider a shift assignment problem, a production planning problem, and a real-world routing problem, respectively. Across these case studies, modeling both perceived costs and decision rules leads to better predictions, highlighting the value of the proposed framework and its greater flexibility in capturing and replicating expert decision making.

</details>


### [198] [Fast Gradient Algorithm with Dry-like Friction and Nonmonotone Line Search for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05653)
*Lien T. Nguyen,Andrew Eberhard,Xinghuo Yu,Chaojie Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于Hilbert空间中可微（可能非凸）函数最小化的快速梯度算法，通过将凸函数的干摩擦性质扩展为非凸场景下的"类干摩擦"性质，并结合线搜索技术自适应更新参数。算法在适当的参数选择下可收敛到目标函数的临界点或"近似"临界点，并在Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz（KL）性质下建立完全的序列收敛性。该算法可以退化为多种现有的带Hessian阻尼和干摩擦的惯性梯度算法，并通过Moreau包络的变分性质扩展到弱凸非光滑优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的惯性梯度算法虽在凸优化中表现良好，但在非凸问题中缺乏统一的理论框架和灵活的算法设计。干摩擦性质在凸函数下已有研究，但尚未推广到非凸场景。此外，非光滑优化问题的处理也需要更通用的框架。

Method: 1) 将凸函数的干摩擦性质扩展为"类干摩擦"性质，适用于非凸可微函数。2) 引入线搜索技术，在每次迭代中自适应更新参数。3) 利用Moreau包络的变分性质，将算法推广到弱凸非光滑优化问题。4) 证明了KL函数Moreau包络的KL指数结果从凸函数推广到更广泛的（不必然凸或连续）KL函数类。

Result: 算法在适当参数选择下具有子序列收敛到临界点或全序列收敛到"近似"临界点的保证；在merit函数的KL性质下建立了全序列收敛到临界点的结果。算法可退化出多种现有算法（带Hessian阻尼和干摩擦的惯性梯度算法），并在弱凸非光滑问题上得到扩展。仿真实验验证了算法效率，并展示了类干摩擦结合外推与线搜索的潜在优势。

Conclusion: 该工作为Hilbert空间中的非凸可微及弱凸非光滑优化提供了一种统一且灵活的算法框架，通过类干摩擦性质与线搜索的结合，在理论上保证了收敛性并兼容了多种已有算法，实验证明了其有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a fast gradient algorithm for the problem of minimizing a differentiable (possibly nonconvex) function in Hilbert spaces. We first extend the dry friction property for convex functions to what we call the dry-like friction property in a nonconvex setting, and then employ a line search technique to adaptively update parameters at each iteration. Depending on the choice of parameters, the proposed algorithm exhibits subsequential convergence to a critical point or full sequential convergence to an ``approximate'' critical point of the objective function. We also establish the full sequential convergence to a critical point under the Kurdyka--Lojasiewicz (KL) property of a merit function. Thanks to the parameters' flexibility, our algorithm can reduce to a number of existing inertial gradient algorithms with Hessian damping and dry friction. By exploiting variational properties of the Moreau envelope, the proposed algorithm is adapted to address weakly convex nonsmooth optimization problems. In particular, we extend the result on KL exponent for the Moreau envelope of a convex KL function to a broad class of KL functions that are not necessarily convex nor continuous. Simulation results illustrate the efficiency of our algorithm and demonstrate the potential advantages of combining dry-like friction with extrapolation and line search techniques.

</details>


### [199] [Curvature Residual Geometry in Bregman Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05680)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类通过最小化Bregman损失拟合的线性回归模型，揭示了即使生成势函数φ是强凸的，其对应的回归目标函数仍可能是非凸的。作者将Hessian矩阵表示为加权Gram矩阵，其权重依赖于势函数的导数和当前残差，由此给出了局部强凸性、光滑性以及梯度下降条件线性收敛的充分条件。针对二次-四次势函数，推导了精确的标量凸性条件，识别了负曲率区间，并通过数值实验表明标量条件可能过紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Bregman回归中，生成势函数的凸性通常被期望直接保证回归目标的凸性。但本文指出，即使φ是强凸的，回归目标函数在θ上仍可能是非凸的，这造成了生成势的凸性与优化几何之间的"缺口"。理解这一缺口对于分析和优化此类回归模型至关重要。

Method: 将Hessian矩阵重写为加权Gram矩阵，其权重由势函数的一阶、二阶导数及当前残差决定；基于此表示推导局部强凸性、光滑性和梯度下降条件线性收敛的条件；针对二次-四次势φ(t)=t²/2+λt⁴/4进行精确标量分析，并通过数值实验验证和对比。

Result: 对二次-四次势函数，推导出精确的标量凸性条件，识别了负曲率区间，给出了局部和全局正曲率充分条件。数值实验表明，标量条件可能过于保守——即使标量条件不满足，完整Hessian在某些评估点上仍保持正定；同时，随四次项参数增大，可行的梯度下降步长范围收窄。

Conclusion: 这些结果刻画了残差依赖的曲率如何与生成势函数和设计矩阵在逆向Bregman回归中相互作用，为理解和优化此类非凸回归问题提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We consider linear regression models fitted by minimizing Bregman losses of the form \[ \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^n \left[ φ(y_i)-φ(x_i^\topθ) -φ'(x_i^\topθ) \bigl(y_i-x_i^\topθ\bigr) \right], \] where \(y_i\) is the observed response and \(x_i^\topθ\) is the linear prediction. Even when the generating potential \(φ\) is strongly convex, the resulting regression objective may be nonconvex in \(θ\). This creates a gap between the convexity of the generating potential and the optimization geometry of the fitted model.
  The Hessian can be written as a weighted Gram matrix whose weights depend on the derivatives of the potential and the current residuals. This representation gives simple conditions for local strong convexity, smoothness, and conditional linear convergence of gradient descent.
  For the quadratic--quartic potential, we derive an exact scalar convexity condition, identify the interval of negative curvature, and obtain local and global sufficient conditions for positive curvature. Numerical experiments indicate that these scalar conditions may fail while the full Hessian remains positive definite at the evaluated points. They also indicate that the range of tested gradient-descent step sizes leading to convergence decreases as the quartic parameter grows.
  These results characterize how residual-dependent curvature interacts with the generating potential and the design matrix in reverse Bregman regression.

</details>


### [200] [A Damped Subspace Splitting Algorithm for Constrained Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05682)
*Yuanming Su,Yukuan Hu,Xin Liu,Guanghui Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出DASSP算法，一种用于约束密度泛函理论（CDFT）计算的单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法。通过子空间分裂重构将Stiefel流形约束与二次约束解耦，DASSP成为首个具有严格收敛保证的CDFT算法，数值实验表明其兼具高可行性精度与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDFT在描述电子激发态和电荷局域态方面具有重要物理化学价值，但其离散优化问题同时包含Stiefel流形约束和非凸二次约束，现有算法要么无法高精度满足二次约束，要么因双循环迭代结构面临收敛困难，亟需一种高效且具有严格收敛保证的求解算法。

Method: 首先利用旋转不变性引入非线性子空间对齐约束，推导出子空间分裂重构，将两组约束解耦；基于该重构提出单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法（DASSP），每步迭代包含谱最小化、投影梯度和阻尼对偶上升三个子步骤，均可高效实现。

Result: 在合成及真实CDFT问题上的数值结果表明，DASSP能同时达到高可行性精度和良好效率，且在鲁棒性方面表现优异；作为首个具有严格收敛保证的CDFT算法，验证了其在实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: DASSP为CDFT计算提供了一种可靠且高效的解决方案，克服了现有方法在约束满足和收敛性方面的局限，有望推动大规模CDFT应用的可靠实现。

Abstract: Constrained density functional theory (CDFT) provides a powerful framework for describing electronically excited and charge-localized states, which underlie a broad range of physical and chemical phenomena. However, the discretized optimization problems arising from CDFT calculations remain challenging, owing to the presence of both the Stiefel manifold constraint and additional nonconvex quadratic constraints. Existing algorithms either fail to enforce the quadratic constraints with high accuracy or face convergence issues due to double-loop iterative structures. In this paper, we first derive a subspace-splitting reformulation that decouples the two groups of constraints, by exploiting the inherent rotation invariance and introducing a nonlinear subspace alignment constraint. Based on this reformulation, we propose a single-loop damped alternating direction method of multipliers, called DASSP. To the best of our knowledge, DASSP is the first algorithm for CDFT calculations with rigorous convergence guarantees. Each iteration of DASSP comprises a spectral minimization step, a projected gradient step, and a damped dual ascent step, all of which admit efficient implementations. Numerical results on synthetic and realistic CDFT problems demonstrate that DASSP attains high feasibility accuracy and exhibits favorable efficiency without compromising robustness. We expect that this work will pave the way toward reliable and efficient large-scale CDFT applications.

</details>


### [201] [Quadratic Degree Sequence Optimization and the Critical Roots of a Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05827)
*Frédéric Meunier,Shmuel Onn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了度序列优化问题在二次函数情形下的复杂性，引入了图的临界根概念，并证明了最优值随二次根变化的函数是凸的分片仿射函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度序列优化问题是一个基础组合优化问题，旨在给定图中寻找一个子图使得所有顶点上某个给定函数（基于子图度数）的和最大化。理解该问题在二次函数情形下的复杂度结构和最优值的行为特性，对于组合优化和图论的理论研究具有重要意义。

Method: 通过引入"临界根"（critical roots）这一新概念，对二次函数情形下的度序列优化问题进行分析。利用临界根将二次根参数的取值空间划分为若干区间，在每个区间上研究最优值的结构特性。

Result: 证明了对于二次函数情形，当二次根变化时，问题的最优值是凸的分片仿射函数，并且这些分片区间的边界恰好由图的临界根确定。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了度序列优化问题在二次函数情形下的结构性刻画，表明最优值函数具有良好的凸性及分片线性性质，为深入理解该问题的复杂度提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The degree sequence optimization problem is to find a subgraph of a given graph which maximizes the sum over all vertices of a given function evaluated at the subgraph degree of that vertex. Here we study this problem and its complexity for quadratic functions. In particular, we introduce the critical roots of a graph, and show they define intervals over which the optimal value of the problem, as the quadratic root varies, is convex piecewise affine.

</details>


### [202] [Robust priority-aware coverage optimization for aerial sensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05873)
*Vanshika Datta,C. Nahak,J. C. Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向空中传感器网络的优先级感知鲁棒覆盖优化框架，在传感器位置不确定条件下，通过RRF鲁棒性公式化约束建立数学模型，并设计了优先级感知鲁棒方向优化（PAROO）算法来确定传感器朝向，以最大化加权覆盖目标。在机场监控场景实验中，该框架能有效引导感知资源向高优先级区域倾斜，并优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中传感器网络在实际部署中存在传感器位置不确定性，而现有覆盖优化方法往往未同时考虑区域优先级差异和位置扰动鲁棒性，难以满足安全敏感环境中对重点区域优先覆盖的实际需求。

Method: 首先构建一个融合监控约束和基于RRF的鲁棒性公式化的数学优化模型；随后提出优先级感知鲁棒方向优化（PAROO）算法，用于求解使加权覆盖目标最大化的传感器朝向配置。

Result: 在机场监控场景的实验结果显示，所提框架能够有效将感知资源导向高优先级区域，并获得比代表性基线方法更高的加权覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该框架在安全敏感环境下具有实际应用价值，能够在位置不确定性条件下实现优先级感知的鲁棒覆盖优化。

Abstract: This article presents a priority-aware robust coverage optimization framework for an aerial sensor network under sensor location uncertainty. Each region is assigned a priority weight, and the objective is to maximize the weighted coverage while maintaining robustness against positional perturbations. A mathematical optimization model is developed by incorporating surveillance constraints and an RRF-based robustness formulation into the proposed framework. An efficient priority-aware robust orientation optimization (PAROO) algorithm is then proposed to determine the sensor orientations that maximize the weighted coverage objective. Experimental results on an airport-inspired surveillance scenario demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively directs sensing resources toward high-priority regions and achieves higher weighted coverage than representative baseline approaches, highlighting its practical applicability in security-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [203] [Exact Anchoring and a Dualization-Based Matheuristic for Bi-Level Dual-Defense Network Interdiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05922)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为双层双防御攻击者模型（BDAM）提供精确最优解，通过下层对偶化将主导攻击者路径项转化为单层混合整数规划，在18个基准算例上全部获得最优性认证（17个在10秒内），并指出供应规则在平局下不适定，提出平局不变的风险走廊公式化方法，最终MILP-DA混合框架在大多数算例上优于此前已发表的元启发式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层阻断模型常用元启发式求解，但在测试规模下无法获得精确最优解，导致解质量无法评估。本文旨在为BDAM提供精确最优解，以评估此前元启发式的性能并改进求解方法。

Method: 第一，通过下层对偶化实现攻击者路径项的单层精确重构，得到可证明最优性的混合整数规划；第二，提出平局不变的威胁走廊公式化以解决供应规则在最短路平局下的不适定问题；第三，构建MILP-DA框架，将精确锚点与走廊解码及可行性修复相结合。

Result: 18个三行和五行基准算例全部获得最优性认证，17个在10秒内完成；15*30网格上获得强未认证可行解。已发表的元启发式平均低3.55%且差距随规模扩大。平局变化对目标影响小于10^(-3)，但实现供应成本最大偏移0.83单位。MILP-DA在13/18个已认证算例和全部18个更大算例上取胜，失败均在三行网格。

Conclusion: BDAM可获得精确求解，元启发式此前存在显著且随规模扩大的最优性差距；平局歧义被目标权重抑制而非真正消除；MILP-DA混合方法总体上优于纯元启发式，但三行网格场景仍需关注。

Abstract: Bi-level interdiction models are frequently solved by metaheuristics whose solution quality cannot be assessed, because exact optima are unavailable at the scales tested. We supply them for the bi-level dual-defense attacker model (BDAM), which couples node interdiction, edge destruction and capacitated supply support, and which we previously solved by a hybrid metaheuristic. First, BDAM's dominant attacker-path term admits an exact single-level reformulation by lower-level dualization, a reduction available whenever arc lengths are linear in the defender's binary decisions; the resulting mixed-integer program certifies optimality on all eighteen three-row and five-row configurations of our earlier benchmark, seventeen in under ten seconds, with strong uncertified incumbents out to 15*30 grids. Our published averages sit 3.55% below that frontier, and the gap widens with scale. Second, the supply rule, like any rule priced on a single attacker shortest path, is ill-posed under ties, and our threat-corridor formulation is tie-invariant by construction. The tie-break moves the objective by under 10^(-3) but the realized supply cost by up to 0.83 units, so the defect is suppressed by the objective weight rather than absent. Third, MILP-DA pairs the exact anchor with the corridor decode and a feasibility repair; compared against our published figures with no re-implementation on either side, it wins on thirteen of eighteen certified configurations and on all eighteen larger ones, every loss falling on a three-row grid.

</details>


### [204] [Stabilizer Design for Policy Iteration in Stochastic Linear Quadratic Control: A Spectrum-Assignment Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05953)
*Xinyu Cao,Bing-Chang Wang,Ying Cao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间不定随机线性二次控制中的模型无关策略迭代（PI）初始化问题，提出了一种基于Lyapunov算子谱的新的谱分配方法，通过累积因子逐步从稳定辅助系统逼近原始系统获得初始稳定控制器，并设计了不依赖初始稳定策略的模型无关算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略迭代（PI）是解决最优控制问题的重要强化学习工具，但其初始化阶段需要寻找初始稳定控制器，且通常依赖完整模型信息，这给模型无关PI的初始化带来巨大限制。对于含状态和控制相关乘性噪声的随机系统，稳定性由包含漂移项和扩散项的Lyapunov型不等式保证而非确定性情形中的Hurwitz条件，使得对应的模型无关PI初始化问题更具挑战性。

Method: 提出了一种新的谱分配方法：借助Lyapunov型算子的谱，通过调整累积因子逐步从稳定辅助系统逼近原始系统，从而获得稳定控制增益；进一步利用系统数据并调整累积因子，设计了不依赖初始稳定策略的模型无关算法以实现最优控制。

Result: 通过仿真结果验证了所提出方法在获得初始稳定控制器和实现最优控制方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了含乘性噪声随机系统中模型无关策略迭代的初始化难题，为连续时间不定随机线性二次控制提供了一种无需初始稳定策略的可行方案。

Abstract: Policy iteration (PI) is an important reinforcement learning tool for solving optimal control problems which includes an initialization stage, i.e., the search for an initial stabilizing controller. However, the initialization stage typically relies on complete model information, thereby imposing substantial constraints on the initialization of model-free PI. For stochastic systems with multiplicative noise dependent on state and control, the stability is not ensured by Hurwitz conditions as in the deterministic case, but rather by a Lyapunov-type inequality that incorporates both drift and diffusion terms. Therefore, the corresponding model-free PI initialization problem is more challenging. To this end, a novel spectrum assignment method is proposed to obtain an initial stabilizer for PI in continuous-time indefinite stochastic linear quadratic control. With the help of the Lyapunov-type operator's spectrum, the original system is gradually approximated from the stable auxiliary system by adjusting a cumulative factor, thereby obtaining a stabilizing control gain. Furthermore, by leveraging system data and adjusting the cumulative factor, we design a model-free algorithm that does not rely on an initial stabilizing policy and can achieve optimal control. Finally, simulation results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [205] [Distributed Fault Diagnosis in Discrete Event Systems with Transmission Delay Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06035)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究部分可观测离散事件系统中的分布式故障诊断问题，提出了一种新的分布式可诊断性条件，该条件扩展了已有文献中的分散式可诊断性条件，并通过一种名为"延迟记录器"的新型结构和新的诊断函数来验证该条件，以应对传输延迟对故障诊断的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式故障诊断方法未充分考虑传输延迟对诊断性能的影响，而实际系统中延迟是不可避免的，因此需要一种能够在存在传输延迟的情况下验证故障是否可在有限步内被诊断的方法。

Method: 提出了扩展分散式可诊断性条件的新的分布式可诊断性条件；构建了一种名为"延迟记录器"（delay recorder）的新型结构，并设计了新的诊断函数来对分布式可诊断性条件进行验证。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的验证方法能够成功判定故障是否可被诊断，即在存在传输延迟的情况下仍能有效确定系统的分布式可诊断性。

Conclusion: 本文为部分可观测离散事件系统在存在传输延迟情况下的分布式故障诊断问题提供了一种新的可诊断性条件及验证方法，弥补了现有分散式诊断方法无法处理延迟问题的不足，理论上证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: This note studies the distributed fault diagnosis problem in partially-observed discrete event systems, where the system is monitored by a group of agents to cooperatively diagnose faults within a finite number of steps. The novelty of this work is the creation of a methodology to verify when the faults can be diagnosed even in the presence of transmission delay impairments. To address this scenario, a new distributed diagnosability condition is proposed, which extends decentralized diagnosability conditions proposed in the literature. Such distributed diagnosability condition is then verified via a novel structure named delay recorder and a new diagnosis function. Theoretical analysis shows that the verification method can successfully determine whether the faults can be diagnosed.

</details>


### [206] [New Lower Bounds for Weak Limited Augmented Zarankiewicz Numbers in the $m\times 3$ Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06050)
*Liqun Qi,Yannan Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文精确确定了弱有限增广Zarankiewicz数$z_{wL}(m,3)$在$m=5,6,7,8,9$时的值，全部等于$2m$，为$z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$（对所有$m\ge5$）的猜想提供了强有力支持，并由此给出BSR$(m,3)\ge2m$的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定弱有限增广Zarankiewicz数$z_{wL}(m,3)$在$m=5,6,7,8,9$时的精确值，验证并支持$z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$对所有$m\ge5$成立的猜想，同时通过该结果推导出BSR$(m,3)$的下界。

Method: 下界通过显式构造弱可容许（weak admissible）的图例给出；上界（对于$m=7,8,9$）通过对正则化$C_4$-free基图进行精确有限搜索获得。

Result: 确定了$z_{wL}(5,3)=10$, $z_{wL}(6,3)=12$, $z_{wL}(7,3)=14$, $z_{wL}(8,3)=16$, $z_{wL}(9,3)=18$，即对这些$m$均有$z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$；并推出BSR$(m,3)\ge2m$（$m=5,6,7,8,9$）。

Conclusion: 该结果与已知的小情况共同强烈支持猜想$z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$对所有$m\ge5$成立，同时为BSR$(m,3)$提供了改进的下界。

Abstract: We determine the exact weak limited augmented Zarankiewicz numbers $z_{wL}(m,3)$ for $m=5,6,7,8,9$. We establish \[ z_{wL}(5,3)=10,\quad z_{wL}(6,3)=12,\quad z_{wL}(7,3)=14,\quad z_{wL}(8,3)=16,\quad z_{wL}(9,3)=18. \] Thus $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ for all $5\le m\le 9$. The lower bounds are given by explicit weak admissible constructions, while the matching upper bounds for $m=7,8,9$ are obtained by exact finite searches over canonical $C_4$-free base graphs. Together with the known small cases, this strongly supports the conjecture that \[ z_{wL}(m,3)=2m \quad \text{for all } m\ge 5. \] Since BSR$(m,n)\ge z_{wL}(m,n)$, these constructions imply \[ {\rm BSR}(m,3)\ge 2m \quad \text{for } m=5,6,7,8,9. \]

</details>


### [207] [On Same-Sample and Independent-Sample Stochastic Extragradient for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06182)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机额外梯度（SEG）方法在单调变分不等式问题（VIPs）上的行为，重点解决了同样本SEG（S-SEG）与独立样本SEG（I-SEG）两种变体在非紧致域、非均匀方差等放宽条件下的收敛性问题，并指出了现有文献中S-SEG理论分析的空白及其在非对称双步长下的失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 额外梯度方法是VIPs的基础算法且有成熟的确定性收敛理论，但其随机版本的收敛性研究相对不足。现有分析大多局限于独立样本SEG，且假设域紧致或随机算子方差均匀有界；同样本SEG作为一种性质显著不同的自然变体，受到的关注远少。本文旨在填补这些理论空白。

Method: 首先证明S-SEG对样本级Lipschitz参数敏感，仅凭均值Lipschitz性和有界方差不足以保证收敛（即使在紧致集上）。然后，针对可能无界的域，在放宽的假设条件下建立两种SEG变体的高概率受限间隙（restricted-gap）收敛性，并证明这些结果在一般情况下无法进一步改进。最后，构造一个随机单调VIP实例，证明已知能保证I-SEG几乎必然最后一迭代收敛的非对称双步长选择在S-SEG下会以几乎必然概率发散。

Result: （1）S-SEG在仅满足均值Lipschitz性和有界方差时在紧致集上也可能不收敛；（2）在无界域下建立了SEG变体的高概率受限间隙收敛界，并证明了其最优性下界；（3）发现非对称双步长策略对I-SEG有效但无法推广到S-SEG，后者存在几乎必然发散的单调VIP实例。

Conclusion: S-SEG与I-SEG在随机收敛性质上存在本质差异；S-SEG需要更严格的假设才能保证收敛，且现有的I-SEG改进策略不能直接迁移到S-SEG。这为随机变分不等式算法的设计提供了重要指导，提示需要针对S-SEG开发专门的步长策略和理论框架。

Abstract: We study stochastic extragradient (SEG) methods for solving monotone variational inequality problems (VIPs) over a feasible set. Although extragradient is a foundational algorithm for VIPs and its deterministic convergence theory is well developed, its stochastic counterpart remains less understood. Most existing analyses focus on independent-sample SEG (I-SEG) and assume either that the domain is compact or that the variance of the stochastic operator is uniformly bounded. The behavior of same-sample SEG (S-SEG), a natural variant with materially different properties, has received far less attention. In this work, we address these gaps in the literature. We first show that S-SEG is sensitive to samplewise Lipschitz parameters: mean Lipschitzness and bounded variance alone do not ensure convergence, even on a compact set. Then, for possibly unbounded domains, we establish a high-probability restricted-gap convergence for each SEG variant under a relaxed set of assumptions, and show that certain fundamental improvements to these results are impossible in general. Finally, we show that a known asymmetric double step-size selection that guarantees almost sure last-iterate convergence for I-SEG can fail for S-SEG: there exists a stochastic monotone VIP for which S-SEG diverges almost surely even under the modified step-sizes.

</details>


### [208] [Characteristic Sensitivity Ensembles for Inference of Hidden Dynamics from Marginal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06190)
*Qi Wang,Gustaaf Jacobs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于推断广义常微分方程组动态的框架，采用随机梯度方法，通过观测到的边缘概率密度函数推断动力学，将扩散等不可逆过程重构为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，利用特征线表示和U统计量构造无偏梯度估计器，并通过四个实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低维状态下观测到的扩散等不可逆随机过程难以直接进行动力学推断，传统方法在处理高维联合密度方程时计算代价高昂。需要一种能够从边缘观测中恢复确定性ODE的有效推断方法。

Method: 将随机或不可逆动力学重构为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，使联合密度满足双曲Liouville方程；利用特征线表示而非直接求解高维Liouville方程；通过沿特征线的灵敏度传播获得欧拉灵敏度，并用交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计器，实现随机梯度下降。

Result: 通过四个实验验证了方法：三模态线性系统通过单模态边缘观测的恢复、含隐藏模态的非线性Gompertz生长模型、使单峰边缘变双峰的双稳态系统、以及细胞流中粒子的Stokes-Oseen阻力定律恢复，并在各设置下分析了收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将不可逆或随机动力学的推断问题转化为从边缘观测中恢复确定性ODE的问题，特征线表示与U统计量相结合为高维问题提供了计算可行的推断方案。

Abstract: A framework is developed for the inference of dynamics described by a generalized system of ordinary differential equations. A stochastic gradient method is coined that infers dynamics from observed marginal probability density functions using the joint probability density function of the observable and latent variables. Diffusion and other irreversible processes observed in a low-dimensional state can be recast as deterministic, reversible flows in a sufficiently augmented state space, where the joint density satisfies the hyperbolic Liouville equation. The marginal distribution observed is the projection of these hyperbolic dynamics onto the observed coordinates, with the latent components carrying the randomness and memory. This reframing allows inference for irreversible or stochastic dynamics into the recovery of a deterministic Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) from marginal observations. Instead of solving the high-dimensional Liouville equation for the joint density, the algorithm exploits its characteristic representation. Particles sampled from the initial distribution are transported along characteristic lines. The Eulerian sensitivity with respect to parameters is obtained by sensitivity propagation along the characteristic lines, with a crossed U-statistic producing an unbiased gradient estimator, which enables stochastic gradient descent. Four experiments validate the method: recovery of a three-mode linear system observed through the marginal of a single mode; a nonlinear Gompertz growth model with a hidden mode; a bistable system whose hidden mode turns a unimodal marginal bimodal; and Stokes--Oseen drag law recovery for particles in a cellular flow. Convergence behavior is analyzed across these settings.

</details>


### [209] [Muon on the Stiefel Manifold Admits an Exact Closed-Form Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06218)
*Mikhail Solonko,Molozhavenko Alexander,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化方法在Stiefel流形（正交列矩阵流形）上的应用，提出了Skewon算法。作者证明了Stiefel Muon更新存在精确的闭式解，基于此开发了高效的Skewon优化算法，并为光滑非凸场景建立了Skewon的一阶收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon是一种矩阵感知的优化方法，其在Stiefel流形（由正交列矩阵构成的流形）上的现有扩展依赖于启发式、近似或迭代更新，计算效率各异且缺乏理论基础。需要一种精确、高效且有收敛保证的Stiefel流形优化方法。

Method: 作者首先证明了Stiefel Muon更新 admits 一个精确的闭式解，利用这一理论结果设计了Skewon算法，实现了高效的正交约束优化，并建立了在光滑非凸设定下的一阶收敛性证明。

Result: 提出了Skewon算法，具有高效的实现方式，并且在光滑非凸环境下具备一阶收敛保证。

Conclusion: Skewon为Stiefel流形上的Muon优化提供了精确闭式解和高效实现，同时具备理论收敛保障，优于现有的启发式和近似方法。

Abstract: We study Muon, a recently proposed matrix-aware optimization method, in the context of the Stiefel manifold. This manifold consists of matrices with orthonormal columns and is ubiquitous in machine learning and scientific computing. Existing extensions of Muon to this manifold rely on heuristic, approximate, or iterative updates with varying computational efficiency. We show that the corresponding Stiefel Muon update admits an exact closed-form solution and use this result to develop Skewon, a practical algorithm for orthogonality-constrained optimization with an efficient implementation. We further establish first-order convergence guarantees for Skewon in the smooth non-convex setting.

</details>


### [210] [Localized Stabilization of Transport PDEs by Interior Flux Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06249)
*Constantinos Kitsos,Ian Manchester*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有界域上多维连续性方程（含源项）的局部内部通量反馈镇定问题。反馈通过通量散度施加，使误差满足带局部阻尼的输运方程。在有限时间特征阻尼不等式及增益条件下，证明了误差在L2空间上的指数稳定性；同时给出了加权Lyapunov泛函导出的ISS估计、椭圆右逆通量实现、以及状态非线性依赖速度场的扩展，并以二维示例验证了几何、增益与实现条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多维连续性方程在有界域上的镇定问题，传统的边界或全局反馈在实际中难以实现或不适用，需要寻求局部内部通量反馈策略，同时需要建立严格的稳定性理论框架。

Method: 通过散度算子的局部内部通量反馈构造误差输运方程；基于有限时间特征阻尼不等式这一核心几何条件，结合增益条件（局部阻尼与输运场压缩放大关系）；使用加权Lyapunov泛函建立微分Lyapunov准则和ISS估计；借助椭圆右逆实现通量反馈，并推广至非线性速度场依赖的情形。

Result: 在有限时间特征阻尼和增益条件下实现了误差在L2中的指数稳定性；建立了加权Lyapunov泛函下的输入-状态稳定性（ISS）估计；给出了通量反馈的椭圆右逆实现方法；将方法推广到状态非线性依赖的速度场；通过二维数值示例验证了几何条件、增益条件和实现条件。

Conclusion: 本文建立了基于局部内部通量反馈的多维连续性方程镇定的完整理论框架，涵盖指数稳定性、ISS估计、反馈实现及非线性推广，为实际工程中的分布式参数系统镇定提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study stabilization of multidimensional continu- ity equations with source terms on bounded domains by means of localized interior flux feedback. The feedback is prescribed through the divergence of the flux and is chosen so that the error with respect to a reference profile satisfies a transport equation with localized damping. The main geometric condition is a finite-time characteristic damping inequality, requiring relevant characteristics to accumulate a uniform amount of damping over a time horizon. This condition is shown to yield exponential stability in L2 of the error, under a gain condition relating localized damping to compressive amplification of the transport field. Lyapunov-type entrance conditions ensure characteris- tic damping on support-restricted families of trajectories. A weighted Lyapunov functional provides a differential Lyapunov criterion and an input-to-state (ISS) estimate with respect to additive perturbations. We also discuss elliptic right-inverse realizations of the feedback flux and extend the characteristic damping argument to velocity fields depending nonlinearly on the state. A two-dimensional example finally illustrates the geometric, gain, and realization conditions.

</details>


### [211] [Fault Diagnosis and Prognosis in Partially-Observed Discrete Event Systems with Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06295)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散事件系统中观测可能延迟到达场景下的故障诊断与预测问题，提出了针对延迟观测的诊断性和可预测性条件，并通过引入延迟观测器和新的验证函数来验证这些条件，理论分析表明该方法有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际系统中观测信息往往存在通信延迟，传统离散事件系统的故障诊断与预测假设观测即时到达，无法直接适用于延迟场景。现有的可预测性概念在此场景下过于保守，需要进行修订。

Method: 提出了两个针对延迟观测的诊断性和可预测性条件，修订了状态-of-the-art的可预测性定义以避免保守性，引入了延迟观测器来构建系统的观测模型，并设计了一个新的验证函数来检验所提出的条件。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的验证方法对于延迟观测场景下的故障诊断与预测是有效的。

Conclusion: 本文为延迟观测下的离散事件系统故障诊断与预测提供了系统的验证框架，同时指明了现有可预测性概念在延迟场景下的局限性并加以修正。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis and prognosis in discrete event systems are studied in the scenario where the observations are possibly received with delay. To address this scenario, two conditions for diagnosis and prognosis with delayed observations are proposed, where we show that the state-of-the-art notion of prognosability must be revised to avoid conservativeness. Diagnosability and prognosability conditions are then verified by introducing a delay observer and a new verification function. Theoretical analysis indicates the effectiveness of the verification method for fault diagnosis and prognosis in the system.

</details>


### [212] [The Benefits of an Integrated Approach for Stability-Constrained Power System Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06349)
*Gereon Recht,Benedikt Jahn,Oussama Alaya,Karl-Kiên Cao,Hendrik Lens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了电力系统规划中一种集成方法，即在发电、输电和储能扩展规划中同时考虑稳定化措施（通过惯性及电压稳定的简化约束），而非传统的顺序规划方法。结果显示集成方法可降低系统成本，并且电网构网型电池储能系统的双重用途优于静态同步补偿器等其他稳定措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变器接口资源（IBR）在电力系统中渗透率的增加，需要替代同步发电机在动态电压稳定和惯量响应方面的贡献。然而传统的电力系统规划方法本质上是顺序性的，稳定化措施仅在规划后期才被考虑，导致规划方案可能不够高效或经济。

Method: 提出一种集成的电力系统规划方法，将稳定化措施（通过简化的惯量和电压稳定约束来表征）与发电扩展、输电扩展和储能扩展同时纳入规划模型中，并与传统的顺序规划方法进行对比。

Result: 集成规划方法能够降低系统总成本；同时，电网构网型电池储能系统由于其双重用途（既能提供稳定服务又能提供能量存储）比其他稳定化措施（如静态同步补偿器STATCOM）更具优势。

Conclusion: 集成式电力系统规划优于顺序规划，并且电网构网型电池储能系统应被优先考虑作为稳定化措施，因为它能够通过双重用途实现成本效益。

Abstract: Increasing penetration of inverter-based resources in today's power systems requires substitution of the contribution from synchronous generators to dynamic voltage stability and inertial response. However, established approaches for power system planning are sequential, as stabilising measures are only considered at a later stage of the planning process. We investigate the advantages of an integrated approach for power system planning, where stabilising measures are considered simultaneously with the expansion of generation, transmission, and storage systems via simplified stability constraints on inertia and voltage stability. We find that system costs are reduced with the integrated approach and that the dual-use option of grid-forming battery energy storage systems is favoured over other stabilising measures like static synchronous compensators.

</details>
